# 0｜V5.1.0 正式候選定位

本檔是 Lighter V5.1.0 的完整 Ratification Target。

V5.1.0 保留 Lighter 作為 LIHUO Control Plane，
但把 Primary Cycle Identity ownership 與 Governance Authority 正式拆開。

Cross-Stack Integration 已完成；本版不再是手術 RC。


V5.0.x 的 Lighter 實務上同時做兩件事：

```text
A. 建立 Native LIHUO Cycle 身分
B. 治理該 Cycle
```

V5.1.0 將兩者拆開。

```text
CYCLE IDENTITY OWNERSHIP
!=
LIHUO GOVERNANCE AUTHORITY.
```

所以 Lighter V5.1.0 必須同時支援：

```text
NATIVE MODE
→ Lighter 建立 Native LIHUO Governing Cycle
→ Lighter 治理

VERTICAL MODE
→ Vertical / Host 已有合法 Cycle Identity
→ Lighter 不再建立第二本 LIHUO Primary Cycle
→ Lighter 對該 Cycle 執行 LIHUO Governance Admission
→ Lighter 治理
```

本稿不改：

```text
MAIN SOLVES.
LIGHTER RELEASES.
```

也不讓：

```text
VERTICAL OWNER
DIRECTOR
HOST WRAPPER
MAIN
```

取得 Lighter 的 Final Gate / Release 權。

---

# L5.1-0｜Root

```text
PROTOCOL GOVERNS.
LIGHTER QUALIFIES.
LIGHTER ADMITS.
LIGHTER BINDS GOVERNANCE.
LIGHTER GOVERNS DIRTY / STALE / REENTRY.
LIGHTER RUNS FINAL GATE.
LIGHTER RELEASES.

MAIN COMPUTES.

LIGHTER MAY OWN A NATIVE CYCLE IDENTITY.
LIGHTER NEED NOT OWN A VERTICAL CYCLE IDENTITY.

CYCLE IDENTITY OWNERSHIP
!=
GOVERNANCE AUTHORITY.
```

工程根句保留：

```text
DAIL DEFINES.
IR NORMALIZES.
CODING CONNECTS.
RUNTIME EXECUTES.

CODING MAY SCHEDULE.
CODING MAY NOT SOLVE.
```

---

# L5.1-1｜Attempt Admission Boundary

Lighter 不接受 payload 自鑄 Attempt standing。

```yaml
LIGHTER_ATTEMPT_ADMISSION:

  consumes:
    - ATTEMPT_REF
    - AUTHORIZED_ORIGIN_REF
    - ORIGIN_CLASS
    - SCOPE_REF
    - OPENING_SNAPSHOT_REF
    - PROVENANCE_REF

  allowed_origin_class:
    - NATIVE_LIHUO_GOVERNANCE_ORIGIN
    - AUTHORIZED_VERTICAL_OR_HOST_ORIGIN

  may_not_accept_as_authoritative:
    - PAYLOAD_SELF_MINTED_ATTEMPT
    - ATTEMPT_RENAMING_AS_NEW_ATTEMPT
    - PAYLOAD_SELF_DECLARED_TERMINATION

  pending_state:
    attempt_state: ATTEMPT_PENDING_GOVERNING_CYCLE
    active_primary_required: 0
    generative_progression: FORBIDDEN

  established_state:
    attempt_state: ESTABLISHED_LIHUO_GOVERNED_GENERATION_ATTEMPT
    active_primary_required: EXACTLY_ONE
```

Lighter 可以驗收 Attempt origin。

Lighter 不可以在 Vertical mode 中：

```text
把自己變成 Vertical Identity 的出生機關。
```

# L5.1-2｜Primary Governing Cycle Dual Mode

```yaml
PRIMARY_GOVERNING_CYCLE_MODE:

  NATIVE_LIHUO:
    cycle_identity_owner: LIHUO_LIGHTER
    Lighter_may_mint:
      - GOVERNING_CYCLE_REF
      - NATIVE_CYCLE_ID_ALIAS
    required_class: NATIVE_LIHUO_CYCLE

  AUTHORIZED_VERTICAL:
    cycle_identity_owner: VERTICAL_OR_HOST_AUTHORITY
    Lighter_may_mint_parallel_primary: false
    required_class: AUTHORIZED_VERTICAL_CYCLE
    requires:
      - EXTERNAL_GOVERNING_CYCLE_REF
      - CYCLE_IDENTITY_PROVENANCE_REF
      - VERTICAL_OR_HOST_AUTHORITY_REF
      - ATTEMPT_REF
      - SCOPE_REF
      - CURRENT_SNAPSHOT_REF
```

硬鎖：

```text
VERTICAL CYCLE EXISTS
!=
LIHUO GOVERNANCE ADMITTED.

LIHUO GOVERNANCE ADMITTED
!=
LIGHTER CREATED THE VERTICAL CYCLE.

AUTHORIZED VERTICAL PRIMARY
→
NO PARALLEL NATIVE LIHUO PRIMARY
FOR THE SAME ATTEMPT.
```

# L5.1-2A｜External Vertical Cycle Continuity Governance × Propagation

Vertical admission 不是永久保鮮。

Lighter 不擁有 Vertical Cycle Identity；
但 Lighter 擁有：

```text
ITS OWN GOVERNANCE RELATION
TO THAT EXTERNAL IDENTITY.
```

正式候選：

```yaml
VERTICAL_CYCLE_CONTINUITY_GOVERNANCE:

  applies_when:
    governing_cycle_class: AUTHORIZED_VERTICAL_CYCLE

  lighter_does_not_own:
    - EXTERNAL_CYCLE_IDENTITY
    - VERTICAL_LOCAL_EXISTENCE
    - HOST_IDENTITY_ORIGIN

  lighter_owns:
    - WHETHER_LIHUO_GOVERNANCE_ADMISSION_REMAINS_VALID
    - WHETHER_CURRENT_SCOPE_STILL_MATCHES
    - WHETHER_CURRENT_AUTHORITY_STILL_STANDS
    - WHETHER_IDENTITY_PROVENANCE_IS_STILL_CURRENT
    - WHETHER_SUPERSESSION_OR_REVOCATION_MAKES_ADMISSION_DIRTY_OR_STALE

  continuity_check_points:
    - INITIAL_ADMISSION
    - MATERIAL_SCOPE_EVENT
    - MATERIAL_AUTHORITY_EVENT
    - REENTRY_QUALIFICATION
    - PRE_RELEASE
    - FINAL_GATE

  continuity_state:
    - CURRENT
    - DIRTY
    - STALE
    - REVOKED
    - SUPERSEDED
    - UNEVIDENCED

  continuity_record:
    continuity_qualification_ref: REF_OR_NONE
    identity_standing_ref: REF_OR_NONE
    authority_standing_ref: REF_OR_NONE
    identity_provenance_ref: REF_OR_NONE
    checked_snapshot_ref: REF_OR_NONE

  on_external_revocation_or_supersession:
    allowed_effects:
      - MARK_GOVERNANCE_ADMISSION_DIRTY
      - MARK_GOVERNANCE_ADMISSION_STALE
      - SET_CONTROL_STATE_HOLD
      - REQUIRE_REAUTHORIZATION
      - REQUIRE_TERMINATION_QUALIFICATION

  may_not:
    - IGNORE_EXTERNAL_IDENTITY_REVOCATION
    - TREAT_INITIAL_ADMISSION_AS_PERMANENT
    - SELF_RECREATE_REVOKED_VERTICAL_AUTHORITY
```

根句：

```text
LIGHTER DOES NOT OWN
THE EXTERNAL CYCLE IDENTITY.

BUT LIGHTER OWNS
WHETHER ITS LIHUO GOVERNANCE ADMISSION
REMAINS VALID AGAINST THAT IDENTITY.
```

Continuity propagation law：

```yaml
CONTINUITY_CHANGE_PROPAGATION:

  trigger:
    - MATERIAL_IDENTITY_STANDING_CHANGE
    - MATERIAL_AUTHORITY_STANDING_CHANGE
    - MATERIAL_SCOPE_CHANGE
    - MATERIAL_IDENTITY_PROVENANCE_CHANGE
    - EXTERNAL_PRIMARY_REVOKED
    - EXTERNAL_PRIMARY_SUPERSEDED

  propagate:
    governance_admission:
      - DIRTY_OR_STALE

    qualified_input_package:
      - CURRENT_STANDING_INVALIDATED
      - OR_REQUALIFICATION_REQUIRED

    main_coverage:
      - DIRTY_OR_STALE

    prior_covering_main_return:
      current_coverage_standing: HISTORICAL_ONLY

  recovery:
    lighter_must_qualify:
      - REENTRY_REQUIRED
      - OR_QUALIFIED_NON_REENTRY_BASIS

  non_reentry_allowed_only_if:
    - CONTINUITY_REFRESH_HAS_NO_MATERIAL_EFFECT_ON_SCOPE
    - CONTINUITY_REFRESH_HAS_NO_MATERIAL_EFFECT_ON_SEMANTIC_STANDING
    - CONTINUITY_REFRESH_HAS_NO_MATERIAL_EFFECT_ON_AUTHORITY_RELEVANT_TO_COMPUTE
    - QUALIFIED_NON_REENTRY_BASIS_EXISTS

  forbidden:
    - ADMISSION_RECURRENT_AUTO_REVALIDATES_OLD_QIP
    - ADMISSION_RECURRENT_AUTO_REVALIDATES_OLD_MAIN_RETURN
    - CONTINUITY_CURRENT_AUTO_MEANS_COVERAGE_CURRENT
```

硬鎖：

```text
ADMISSION BECOMES CURRENT AGAIN
!=
OLD MAIN RETURN AUTOMATICALLY BECOMES CURRENT AGAIN.
```

# L5.1-2B｜Non-Cycle Control-Plane Preservation Lock

Cycle identity ownership 解耦不得被解讀成：

```text
LIGHTER V5.1
=
ONLY GOVERNANCE ADMISSION + COVERAGE + GATE.
```

所有未被 V5.1 明示 supersede、且與 Single Governing Cycle 不衝突的
Lighter control-plane 責任繼續保留。

```yaml
LIGHTER_NON_CYCLE_CONTROL_PLANE_PRESERVATION:

  preserved:
    - GLOBAL_LLM_SELF_STANDING_GATE
    - RAW_INPUT_ADMISSION
    - LLIA
    - LLIA_GATE
    - PREMISE_QUALIFICATION
    - AUTHORITY_STANDING_QUALIFICATION
    - ATTENTION_EFFECT_QUALIFICATION
    - EXTERNAL_CONSTRAINT_SEPARATION
    - R_CCC_OBLIGATION_COMPILATION
    - COMPETITION_INTEGRITY_ADJUDICATION
    - QUALIFIED_INPUT_PACKAGE_COMPILATION
    - OUTPUT_CONTRACT_QUALIFICATION
    - OUTPUT_SUFFICIENCY_QUALIFICATION
    - CAPABILITY_ELIGIBILITY_RESOLUTION
    - FUNCTIONAL_MODULE_ORCHESTRATION
    - PROVIDER_TOOL_TRANSPORT_GOVERNANCE
    - EXECUTION_TICKET_GOVERNANCE
    - EVIDENCE_ADMISSION
    - EVIDENCE_ORIGIN_QUALIFICATION
    - RECOVERY_AUTHORIZATION
    - REAUTHORIZATION
    - VERSION_RESOLUTION
    - FINAL_GATE
    - FINAL_RELEASE

  not_transferred_to_vertical:
    - ALL_ABOVE

  not_transferred_to_main:
    - ALL_ABOVE
```

硬鎖：

```text
CYCLE IDENTITY OWNERSHIP DECOUPLING
DOES NOT TRANSFER
NON-CYCLE LIGHTER GOVERNANCE AUTHORITY.
```

尤其：

```text
VERTICAL / HOST AUTHORITY CLAIM
MUST STILL PASS
LIGHTER'S QUALIFICATION BOUNDARIES.

EXTERNAL HOST CLAIM PRESENT
!=
AUTOMATIC LIHUO STANDING.
```

# L5.1-3｜Governance Admission

```yaml
LIHUO_GOVERNANCE_ADMISSION_V5_1:

  owner:
    LIHUO_LIGHTER

  admission_ref: REQUIRED

  binds:
    governing_cycle_ref: REQUIRED
    governing_cycle_class:
      - NATIVE_LIHUO_CYCLE
      - AUTHORIZED_VERTICAL_CYCLE
    generation_attempt_ref: REQUIRED
    scope_ref: REQUIRED
    current_snapshot_ref: REQUIRED
    attempt_origin_ref: REQUIRED
    cycle_identity_provenance_ref: REQUIRED

  semantic_standing:
    - QUALIFIED
    - SEMANTIC_ONLY
    - EVIDENCE_PENDING
    - AUTHORITY_DENIED
    - EXTERNAL_CONSTRAINT
    - DISQUALIFIED

  control_state:
    - OPEN
    - HOLD
    - FAILED
    - CLOSED

  main_coverage_obligation:
    state:
      - NOT_YET_ACTIVE
      - REQUIRED
      - REENTRY_REQUIRED
      - PRECONDITIONS_SATISFIED_FOR_CURRENT_SNAPSHOT

    naming_lock:
      PRECONDITIONS_SATISFIED_FOR_CURRENT_SNAPSHOT:
        means:
          - COVERING_MAIN_RETURN_CURRENT
          - GATE_PRECONDITIONS_MAY_BE_READY
        does_not_mean:
          - MAIN_COVERAGE_CLOSED
          - RELEASE_AUTHORIZED

  provenance:
    requires:
      - RAW_GOVERNANCE_REQUEST_REF
      - PREBOUND_INVOCATION_REF
      - RAW_GOVERNANCE_RETURN_REF_OR_HASH
      - QUALIFIED_CONSUMPTION_REF
```

三分硬鎖：

```text
SEMANTIC STANDING
!=
CONTROL STATE
!=
MAIN COVERAGE OBLIGATION.
```

# L5.1-4｜LLIA-GATE / Qualified Input Package｜Canonical V5.1 Contract

固定主鏈：

```text
RAW INPUT
↓
LLIA-GATE
↓
QUALIFIED_INPUT_PACKAGE_V5_1
↓
MAIN.
```

```yaml
QUALIFIED_INPUT_PACKAGE_V5_1:

  qualified_input_package_ref: REQUIRED
  qualified_input_package_hash: REQUIRED

  inherits:
    - ALL_NON_CONFLICTING_V5_0_1_QIP_FIELDS
    - ALL_NON_CONFLICTING_V5_0_1_QIP_SEMANTICS

  retained_fields_include:
    - AUTHORIZATION_REF
    - SEMANTIC_INPUT
    - PREMISE_ANNOTATIONS
    - GOVERNANCE_STANDING_ANNOTATIONS
    - EXTERNAL_CONSTRAINT_REFS
    - RCCC_CYCLE_OBLIGATION_OR_SUCCESSOR_EQUIVALENT
    - COMPETITION_INTEGRITY_ANNOTATIONS
    - ENGINE_STATE_SEED

  governing_context:
    governing_cycle_ref: REQUIRED
    governing_cycle_class: REQUIRED
    generation_attempt_ref: REQUIRED
    governance_admission_ref: REQUIRED
    scope_ref: REQUIRED
    current_snapshot_ref: REQUIRED

  may_include_standing:
    - QUALIFIED
    - SEMANTIC_ONLY
    - EVIDENCE_PENDING
    - AUTHORITY_DENIED
    - EXTERNAL_CONSTRAINT
    - DISQUALIFIED

  raw_input_direct_to_main:
    forbidden: true

  silent_field_drop:
    forbidden: true
```

送入 Main 時必須成立：

```yaml
QIP_TO_MAIN_EXACT_CONTEXT_BINDING:

  equality:
    - QIP.GOVERNING_CYCLE_REF == MAIN_REQUEST.GOVERNING_CYCLE_REF
    - QIP.GOVERNING_CYCLE_CLASS == MAIN_REQUEST.GOVERNING_CYCLE_CLASS
    - QIP.GENERATION_ATTEMPT_REF == MAIN_REQUEST.GENERATION_ATTEMPT_REF
    - QIP.GOVERNANCE_ADMISSION_REF == MAIN_REQUEST.GOVERNANCE_ADMISSION_REF
    - QIP.SCOPE_REF == MAIN_REQUEST.SCOPE_REF
    - QIP.CURRENT_SNAPSHOT_REF == MAIN_REQUEST.TARGET_SNAPSHOT_REF

  request_must_bind:
    - QUALIFIED_INPUT_PACKAGE_REF
    - QUALIFIED_INPUT_PACKAGE_HASH
```

```text
QUALIFIED_INPUT_PACKAGE_V5_1
=
CURRENT QIP SEMANTICS
+
V5.1 GOVERNING-CYCLE BINDINGS
-
ONLY EXPLICITLY SUPERSEDED FIELDS.
```

# L5.1-5｜Governance Objects｜不再以第二個 cycle_id 承載

Native / Vertical 兩種模式都需要治理物件，但 Vertical mode 不建立第二個 Primary Cycle。

```yaml
LIHUO_GOVERNANCE_OBJECTS_V5_1:

  required:
    - GOVERNANCE_ADMISSION_REF
    - GOVERNANCE_TICKET_REF
    - REQUIRED_EXECUTION_MANIFEST_REF
    - ACCOUNTABILITY_LEDGER_REF
    - SCOPE_REF
    - OPENED_OR_ADMITTED_SNAPSHOT_REF

  primary_identity_anchor:
    - GOVERNING_CYCLE_REF
    - GOVERNING_CYCLE_CLASS
    - GENERATION_ATTEMPT_REF

  native_mode:
    native_cycle_id_alias: MAY_EXIST

  vertical_mode:
    liHuo_cycle_id:
      required: false
    parallel_primary_cycle:
      forbidden: true

  does_not_prove:
    - MAIN_EXECUTED
    - RADAR_EXECUTED
    - FINAL_GATE_EXECUTED
    - FINAL_RELEASE
```

```text
TICKET / MANIFEST / LEDGER
ARE GOVERNANCE OBJECTS.

THEY ARE NOT
A SECOND PRIMARY CYCLE IDENTITY.
```

# L5.1-5A｜Main Invocation Authority Binding Governance

Lighter 不必是 caller，也不創造 Vertical / Host 的存在權。

Lighter 擁有的是：

```text
THE GOVERNANCE BINDING
THAT MAKES THIS EXACT CALL
LAWFUL FOR THIS EXACT MAIN INVOCATION.
```

```yaml
MAIN_INVOCATION_AUTHORITY_BINDING_V5_1:

  governance_owner: LIHUO_LIGHTER

  authority_binding_ref: REQUIRED
  main_invocation_ref: REQUIRED

  caller_ref: REQUIRED
  caller_authority_origin_ref: REQUIRED
  caller_authority_ref: REQUIRED

  target_owner_ref: LIHUO_MAIN

  governing_cycle_ref: REQUIRED
  generation_attempt_ref: REQUIRED
  scope_ref: REQUIRED
  target_snapshot_ref: REQUIRED

  request_ref: REQUIRED
  exact_request_hash: REQUIRED

  qualified_input_package_ref: REQUIRED
  qualified_input_package_hash: REQUIRED

  request_class:
    - INITIAL_MAIN_TRANSIT
    - MAIN_REENTRY

  current_cycle_continuity_qualification_ref:
    required_if: AUTHORIZED_VERTICAL_CYCLE

  issued_before_execution: true

  one_binding_one_invocation: true
```

```text
CALLER AUTHORITY ORIGIN
MAY LIVE OUTSIDE LIGHTER.

LIGHTER QUALIFIES
THE EXACT INVOCATION BINDING.

AUTHORIZED TO CALL MAIN
!=
AUTHORIZED TO QUALIFY OR RELEASE.
```

# L5.1-6｜Main Coverage Obligation Activation

Lighter 沒有 Main-entry 自由裁量。

```text
LIHUO_GOVERNED_GENERATIVE_STANDING ESTABLISHED
+
CONTROL STATE = OPEN
↓
MAIN COVERAGE OBLIGATION = REQUIRED.
```

因此：

```text
LIGHTER DOES NOT DECIDE
WHETHER MAIN IS OPTIONAL.

LIGHTER QUALIFIES THE CONTEXT
IN WHICH THE CONSTITUTIONAL
MAIN COVERAGE OBLIGATION OPERATES.
```

禁止：

```text
Vertical: "I use LIHUO governance but skip Main."
Director: "Main is unnecessary this turn."
Lighter: "I waive Main because output looks simple."
```

# L5.1-7｜Radar Relation

Radar 仍屬 Lighter control plane，不成為 Main predecessor。

```yaml
RADAR_GOVERNING_CYCLE_OBSERVATION_V5_1:

  owner: LIGHTER.RADAR_V6

  resident_on:
    - ESTABLISHED_LIHUO_GOVERNED_PRIMARY_CYCLE

  primary_class_agnostic:
    - NATIVE_LIHUO_CYCLE
    - AUTHORIZED_VERTICAL_CYCLE

  content_authority:
    binding: false
    closure_effect: NONE
    path_winner: false

  trace_requirement:
    - QUALIFIED_OCCURRENCE_STATE
    - OR_QUALIFIED_TRACE_GAP

  missing_or_failed_alone:
    generic_release_veto: false
```

```text
RADAR ADVICE
!=
MAIN REENTRY ORDER.
```

```yaml
  pre_gate_prohibited_requirements:
    - FINAL_RELEASE_DECISION_RECEIPT
    - CLOSED_LEDGER_SNAPSHOT
```

舊 `CYCLE_RECEIPT_LEDGER_V5` 抽象化為：

```yaml

    identity_standing_ref: REF_OR_NONE
    authority_standing_ref: REF_OR_NONE
    identity_provenance_ref: REF_OR_NONE
    checked_snapshot_ref: REF_OR_NONE

  main_coverage:
    obligation_state:
      - NOT_YET_ACTIVE
      - REQUIRED
      - REENTRY_REQUIRED
      - PRECONDITIONS_SATISFIED_FOR_CURRENT_SNAPSHOT

    preconditions_state:
      - NOT_READY
      - READY

    closure_state:
      - OPEN
      - CLOSED_FOR_SNAPSHOT
      - DIRTY
      - STALE

    state_lock:
      PRECONDITIONS_SATISFIED_FOR_CURRENT_SNAPSHOT_NEQ_CLOSED_FOR_SNAPSHOT: true

    latest_covering_main_return_ref: REF_OR_NONE
    covered_snapshot_ref: REF_OR_NONE
    dirty_basis_refs: []
    reentry_governance_ref: REF_OR_NONE

    primary_standing_dependency_lock:
      if_primary_standing_not_CURRENT:
        - PRECONDITIONS_STATE_NOT_READY
        - CLOSURE_STATE_DIRTY_OR_STALE
        - LATEST_COVERING_MAIN_RETURN_CURRENT_STANDING_FORBIDDEN

  final_gate:
    execution_state:
      - NOT_REACHED
      - EXECUTED
      - UNAVAILABLE
      - UNEVIDENCED
    decision:
      - NONE
      - RELEASED
      - HOLD
      - FAILED
    gate_return_ref: REF_OR_NONE
    decision_receipt_ref: REF_OR_NONE

  locks:
    - LEDGER_MAY_NOT_IMPERSONATE_OWNER_RETURN
    - GAP_STATE_MUST_HAVE_QUALIFIED_BASIS
    - NO_GATE_EXECUTION_NEQ_GATE_HOLD
    - HOLD_NEQ_ATTEMPT_TERMINATION
    - FAILED_DECISION_NEQ_AUTOMATIC_ATTEMPT_TERMINATION
```

---

# L5.1-10｜HOLD 不關帳

V5.1 明確禁止：

```text
FINAL GATE HOLD
→
AUTO CLOSE ATTEMPT.
```

```yaml
GATE_HOLD_LIFECYCLE:

  on_final_gate_hold:
    control_state: HOLD
    attempt_terminal: false
    ledger_closed: false

  ledger_phase:
    GATE_HOLD

  progression_while_hold:
    prohibited: true

  may_resume_same_primary_when:
    - HOLD_BASIS_LAWFULLY_RESOLVED
    - REQUIRED_REAUTHORIZATION_COMPLETED
    - REQUIRED_REENTRY_COMPLETED_IF_ANY
    - CONTROL_STATE_REQUALIFIED_TO_OPEN

  later_gate_attempt:
    allowed: true
    requires:
      - NEW_GATE_ATTEMPT_REF
      - CURRENT_PRE_RELEASE_VIEW
      - CURRENT_PROVENANCE

  terminal_transition:
    requires:
      - AUTHORIZED_TERMINATION_REF
      - TERMINATION_PROVENANCE_REF
```

同理：

```text
FINAL GATE FAILED
!=
AUTOMATIC ATTEMPT DEATH.
```

是否終止由獨立 termination standing 決定。

---

# L5.1-10A｜Attempt Termination × Ledger Closure Governance

Vertical Cycle termination 與 LIHUO Attempt termination 不是同一事件。

```yaml
ATTEMPT_TERMINATION_GOVERNANCE_V5_1:

  external_vertical_or_host_authority:
    may_originate:
      - EXTERNAL_CYCLE_CLOSE_EVENT
      - EXTERNAL_CYCLE_REVOKE_EVENT
      - EXTERNAL_CYCLE_SUPERSEDE_EVENT

    may_not_directly_mint:
      - LIHUO_ATTEMPT_TERMINAL_STANDING
      - LIGHTER_LEDGER_CLOSED_STATE
      - FINAL_RELEASE

  lighter:
    owns:
      - QUALIFICATION_OF_EXTERNAL_TERMINATION_EFFECT
      - LIHUO_ATTEMPT_TERMINATION_STANDING
      - GOVERNANCE_ADMISSION_INVALIDATION_OR_CLOSURE
      - ACCOUNTABILITY_LEDGER_CLOSURE_TRANSITION

  liHuo_attempt_termination:
    requires:
      - AUTHORIZED_TERMINATION_REF
      - TERMINATION_PROVENANCE_REF
      - QUALIFIED_TERMINATION_EFFECT_REF

  ledger_close:
    owner: LIHUO_LIGHTER
    requires:
      - QUALIFIED_CLOSURE_BASIS_REF
      - CURRENT_ATTEMPT_STATE
      - CURRENT_GOVERNANCE_STATE
      - CLOSURE_TRANSITION_RECEIPT_OR_RECORD
```

硬鎖：

```text
EXTERNAL CYCLE TERMINATION
!=
LIGHTER INVENTS TERMINATION.

EXTERNAL CYCLE TERMINATION
!=
AUTOMATIC LIHUO FINAL RELEASE.

FINAL GATE HOLD
!=
ATTEMPT TERMINATION.

ATTEMPT TERMINATION
!=
LEDGER CLOSED
UNTIL LIGHTER PERFORMS
THE QUALIFIED CLOSURE TRANSITION.
```

---

# L5.1-10B｜Lost External Primary → Pending Attempt

依 Protocol V5.1 RC2.2 候選法：

```text
ESTABLISHED ATTEMPT
+
CURRENT PRIMARY LOSES LAWFUL STANDING
+
NO REPLACEMENT PRIMARY YET
+
NO TERMINAL STANDING YET
↓
ATTEMPT_PENDING_GOVERNING_CYCLE.
```

Lighter 的責任是承認真實零 Primary，而不是保住一個已失效的假 Primary。

```yaml
LOST_EXTERNAL_PRIMARY_HANDLING:

  trigger:
    - CONTINUITY_STATE_REVOKED
    - CONTINUITY_STATE_SUPERSEDED
    - OR_OTHER_QUALIFIED_LOSS_OF_PRIMARY_STANDING

  if:
    replacement_primary_established: false
    termination_qualified: false

  then:
    attempt_state: ATTEMPT_PENDING_GOVERNING_CYCLE
    active_primary_count: 0
    control_state: HOLD
    generative_progression: FORBIDDEN
    final_gate_eligible: false

    prior_accountability:
      ledger: RETAIN_AS_HISTORY
      main_returns: RETAIN_AS_HISTORY
      old_qip: NOT_CURRENT
      old_main_coverage: NOT_CURRENT

  lawful_exit_paths:

    SAME_PRIMARY_STANDING_RESTORED:
      requires:
        - CURRENT_CONTINUITY_QUALIFICATION
        - GOVERNANCE_READMISSION_OR_REQUALIFICATION
        - QIP_REQUALIFICATION
        - MAIN_COVERAGE_REQUALIFICATION

    SUCCESSOR_PRIMARY_ESTABLISHED:
      requires:
        - TRACEABLE_SUCCESSOR_RELATION
        - NO_PARALLEL_PRIMARY
        - LIHUO_GOVERNANCE_ADMISSION
        - QIP_REBIND_OR_RECOMPILATION
        - MAIN_COVERAGE_REEVALUATION

    TERMINATION_QUALIFIED:
      requires:
        - AUTHORIZED_TERMINATION_REF
        - TERMINATION_PROVENANCE_REF
        - QUALIFIED_TERMINATION_EFFECT_REF
```

硬鎖：

```text
LOST PRIMARY
!=
KEEPING A DEAD PRIMARY ACTIVE.

LOST PRIMARY
!=
AUTOMATIC ATTEMPT TERMINATION.

PENDING
IS THE SAFE ZERO-PRIMARY STATE.
```

---


# L5.1-10C｜Successor Primary within Same Attempt

當 Attempt 已退回 Pending，且合法 successor Primary 建立：

```yaml
SUCCESSOR_PRIMARY_TRANSITION_V5_1:

  generation_attempt_ref:
    stays_same: true

  predecessor_primary:
    old_governing_cycle_ref: HISTORICAL
    old_accountability_ledger_ref: HISTORICAL
    old_main_returns: HISTORICAL_ONLY
    closure_basis: PRIMARY_SUPERSEDED

  successor_primary:
    governing_cycle_ref: NEW_REQUIRED
    governance_admission_ref: NEW_REQUIRED
    governance_ticket_ref: NEW_REQUIRED
    manifest_ref: NEW_REQUIRED
    accountability_ledger_ref: NEW_REQUIRED
    qip_ref: NEW_OR_LAWFULLY_REBOUND
    qip_hash: REQUIRED

  first_main_call:
    request_class: INITIAL_MAIN_TRANSIT

  prohibited:
    - MUTATE_OLD_LEDGER_IDENTITY_INTO_SUCCESSOR
    - TREAT_SUCCESSOR_PRIMARY_AS_SAME_PRIMARY_MAIN_REENTRY
    - CARRY_OLD_MAIN_COVERAGE_AS_CURRENT
```

若只是同一 Primary standing 恢復，才可能依 Lighter qualification 使用：

```text
MAIN_REENTRY.
```

# L5.1-11｜Material Delta / Dirty / Stale / Reentry Governance

Lighter 不擁有 Vertical local semantics，也不擁有 Main compute。

Lighter 擁有的是治理資格。

```yaml
LIGHTER_REENTRY_GOVERNANCE_V5_1:

  consumes:
    - LOCAL_DELTA_REF
    - LOCAL_OWNER_REF
    - CURRENT_SNAPSHOT_REF
    - CURRENT_CYCLE_CONTINUITY_QUALIFICATION_REF_IF_VERTICAL
    - MAIN_COMPUTE_DEPENDENCY_STATE_IF_AVAILABLE
    - CURRENT_MAIN_RETURN_REF_IF_ANY

  may_decide:
    - DIRTY
    - STALE
    - REENTRY_REQUIRED
    - QUALIFIED_NON_REENTRY_BASIS
    - REAUTHORIZATION_REQUIRED
    - NO_CURRENT_QUALIFIED_DECISION

  may_not:
    - INVENT_VERTICAL_DELTA
    - REWRITE_LOCAL_OWNER_FACT
    - PERFORM_MAIN_RECOMPUTE
    - DECLARE_MAIN_COVERAGE_CLOSED_BY_ITSELF
```

一旦：

```text
REENTRY_REQUIRED
```

成立：

```text
MAIN COVERAGE OBLIGATION
→ REENTRY_REQUIRED
```

舊 Main Return 可保留為 history evidence，但不得冒充 current covering Return。

---

# L5.1-12｜Main Coverage Preconditions

Final Gate 入場前，Lighter 必須建立：

```yaml
MAIN_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS_VIEW:

  requires:
    - AT_LEAST_ONE_LAWFUL_MAIN_TRANSIT_EVIDENCED
    - LATEST_RELEASE_RELEVANT_STATE_IDENTIFIED
    - NO_UNRESOLVED_MATERIAL_DIRTY_CAUSE
    - NO_UNRESOLVED_STALE_CAUSE
    - ALL_REQUIRED_REENTRY_CONSUMED
    - LATEST_COVERING_MAIN_RETURN_IDENTIFIED

  result:
    - READY
    - NOT_READY
    - UNEVIDENCED

  ledger_projection:
    UNEVIDENCED:
      preconditions_state: NOT_READY
      qualified_gap_ref: REQUIRED

  READY:
    permits:
      - FINAL_GATE_EXECUTION_ATTEMPT

  does_not_mean:
    - COVERAGE_ALREADY_CLOSED
    - RELEASE_AUTHORIZED
```

硬鎖：

```text
ZERO MAIN
→
PRECONDITIONS NOT READY.
```

---

# L5.1-13｜Main Return Emission Evidence × Lighter Admission Receipt

Main 的 Raw Return 必須先存在，Lighter 才能做 qualified consumption。

```text
MAIN
→ MAIN_ACTUAL_STATE_RETURN
→ MAIN_RETURN_EMISSION_EVIDENCE

LIGHTER
→ QUALIFIED MAIN RETURN CONSUMPTION
→ MAIN_RETURN_ADMISSION_RECEIPT
```

```yaml
MAIN_RETURN_EMISSION_EVIDENCE:
  owner: LIHUO_MAIN

  required_fields:
    - GOVERNING_CYCLE_REF
    - GENERATION_ATTEMPT_REF
    - MAIN_INVOCATION_REF
    - REQUEST_REF
    - EXACT_REQUEST_HASH
    - MAIN_RETURN_REF
    - RAW_MAIN_RETURN_REF_OR_HASH
    - ACTUAL_STATE
    - INPUT_SNAPSHOT_REF
    - COVERED_SNAPSHOT_REF
    - MAIN_OUTPUT_REF
    - EVIDENCE_ORIGIN
```

```yaml
MAIN_RETURN_ADMISSION_RECEIPT_V5_1:

  owner: LIHUO_LIGHTER

  exists_only_when:
    - ACTUAL_MAIN_RETURN_EXISTS
    - MAIN_RETURN_EMISSION_EVIDENCE_EXISTS
    - LIGHTER_QUALIFIED_CONSUMPTION_OCCURRED

  binds:
    - GOVERNING_CYCLE_REF
    - GENERATION_ATTEMPT_REF
    - MAIN_INVOCATION_REF
    - EXACT_MAIN_REQUEST_HASH
    - MAIN_RETURN_EMISSION_EVIDENCE_REF
    - RAW_MAIN_RETURN_REF_OR_HASH
    - QUALIFIED_CONSUMPTION_REF
    - ACTUAL_STATE
    - MAIN_OUTPUT_REF
    - INPUT_SNAPSHOT_REF
    - COVERED_SNAPSHOT_REF

  actual_state:
    - COMPLETE
    - PARTIAL
    - FAILED_STATE

  forbidden:
    - RECEIPT_WITHOUT_MAIN_RETURN
    - RECEIPT_WITHOUT_EMISSION_EVIDENCE
    - PARTIAL_TO_COMPLETE_UPGRADE_BY_CONSUMER
    - MAIN_OUTPUT_REF_SUBSTITUTION
    - FOREIGN_INVOCATION_CAPTURE
```

```text
MAIN NEVER REACHED
→
NO MAIN RETURN
→
NO MAIN EMISSION EVIDENCE
→
NO LIGHTER MAIN RETURN ADMISSION RECEIPT.
```

# L5.1-14｜Pre-Release Accountability View

Final Gate 改讀 Primary Governing Cycle，而不是 native `cycle_id` 專屬 ledger。

```yaml
PRE_RELEASE_ACCOUNTABILITY_VIEW_V5_1:

  requires:
    - GOVERNING_CYCLE_REF
    - GOVERNING_CYCLE_CLASS
    - GENERATION_ATTEMPT_REF
    - GOVERNANCE_ADMISSION_REF
    - EXPECTED_CONTROL_FLOW_OBLIGATIONS
    - CURRENT_STAGE_STATES
    - AVAILABLE_RECEIPT_REFS
    - AVAILABLE_OWNER_RETURN_REFS
    - QUALIFIED_GAP_REFS
    - CURRENT_SCOPE
    - CURRENT_SEMANTIC_STANDING
    - CURRENT_CONTROL_STATE
    - CURRENT_PRIMARY_GOVERNING_CYCLE_STANDING
    - CURRENT_PRIMARY_STANDING_QUALIFICATION_REF
    - CURRENT_GOVERNING_CYCLE_IDENTITY_PROVENANCE
    - CURRENT_VERTICAL_OR_HOST_AUTHORITY_STANDING_IF_VERTICAL
    - CURRENT_CYCLE_CONTINUITY_STATE_IF_VERTICAL
    - CURRENT_CYCLE_CONTINUITY_QUALIFICATION_REF_IF_VERTICAL
    - MAIN_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS_STATE
    - LATEST_COVERING_MAIN_RETURN_REF

  does_not_require:
    - ALL_RECEIPTS_PRESENT
    - FINAL_RELEASE_DECISION_RECEIPT
    - CLOSED_LEDGER_SNAPSHOT
```

---


# L5.1-14A｜Primary Governing Cycle Standing

Final Gate 不要求所有 Primary 都假裝具有 Vertical continuity receipt。

```yaml
PRIMARY_GOVERNING_CYCLE_STANDING_V5_1:

  NATIVE_LIHUO_CYCLE:
    current_basis:
      - LIGHTER_OWNED_NATIVE_CYCLE_ACTIVE_STANDING
      - CURRENT_NATIVE_IDENTITY_PROVENANCE

  AUTHORIZED_VERTICAL_CYCLE:
    current_basis:
      - CURRENT_LEDGERED_CYCLE_CONTINUITY_QUALIFICATION
      - CURRENT_EXTERNAL_IDENTITY_STANDING
      - CURRENT_VERTICAL_OR_HOST_AUTHORITY_STANDING

  output:
    primary_standing_qualification_ref: REQUIRED
    state:
      - CURRENT
      - DIRTY
      - STALE
      - REVOKED
      - SUPERSEDED
      - UNEVIDENCED
```

Gate 只要求：

```text
CURRENT PRIMARY GOVERNING CYCLE STANDING.
```

# L5.1-15｜Final Gate Admission × Coverage Closure

```text
GATE JUDGES MISSING EVIDENCE.
MISSING EVIDENCE DOES NOT ERASE THE GATE.
```

但 Gate 入場需要：

```yaml
LIHUO_FINAL_GATE_V5_1:

  admission_requires:
    - QUALIFIED_PRE_RELEASE_ACCOUNTABILITY_VIEW
    - CURRENT_SCOPE_AND_STANDING
    - CONTROL_STATE_ALLOWS_GATE_ATTEMPT
    - CURRENT_PRIMARY_GOVERNING_CYCLE_STANDING_IS_CURRENT
    - MAIN_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS_READY

  on_execution:
    must_consume:
      - LATEST_COVERING_MAIN_RETURN

    consumption_effect:
      MAIN_COVERAGE_CLOSED_FOR_CURRENT_SNAPSHOT: true

  decision_owner:
    LIHUO_LIGHTER

  decision_after_consumption:
    allowed:
      - RELEASED
      - HOLD
      - FAILED

  RELEASED_requires:
    - MAIN_COVERAGE_CLOSED_FOR_CURRENT_SNAPSHOT
    - CURRENT_PRIMARY_GOVERNING_CYCLE_STANDING_IS_CURRENT
    - NO_UNRESOLVED_RELEASE_VETO
    - RELEASE_CONTRACT_SATISFIED

  standing_refresh_alone:
    may_not:
      - RESTORE_OLD_QIP_CURRENT_STANDING
      - RESTORE_OLD_MAIN_RETURN_CURRENT_COVERAGE_STANDING
      - CLOSE_MAIN_COVERAGE
```

合法順序：

```text
COVERAGE PRECONDITIONS READY
→
GATE EXECUTES
→
GATE CONSUMES LATEST COVERING MAIN RETURN
→
COVERAGE CLOSED FOR CURRENT SNAPSHOT
→
GATE MAY DECIDE RELEASED / HOLD / FAILED.
```

---

# L5.1-16｜Final Gate Execution State / Decision

```yaml
FINAL_GATE_EXECUTION_ATTEMPT_V5_1:

  EXECUTED:
    raw_gate_return: REQUIRED
    decision:
      - RELEASED
      - HOLD
      - FAILED
    distinct_decision_receipt: REQUIRED

  NOT_REACHED:
    decision: NONE
    gate_return: NONE
    decision_receipt: NONE

  UNAVAILABLE:
    decision: NONE
    gate_return: NONE_OR_QUALIFIED_GAP
    decision_receipt: NONE

  UNEVIDENCED:
    decision: NONE
    decision_receipt: NONE
```

硬鎖：

```text
NO FINAL GATE EXECUTION
!=
HOLD.

NO FINAL GATE EXECUTION
!=
FAILED.

HOLD
!=
ATTEMPT TERMINATION.
```

---

# L5.1-17｜Gate Return Provenance

```yaml
FINAL_GATE_PROVENANCE_V5_1:

  requires:
    - RAW_GATE_REQUEST_REF
    - PREBOUND_GATE_INVOCATION_REF
    - EXACT_GATE_REQUEST_HASH
    - RAW_GATE_RETURN_REF_OR_HASH
    - GATE_EVENT_REF
    - DISTINCT_DECISION_RECEIPT_REF
    - QUALIFIED_CONSUMPTION_REF

  locks:
    - RAW_HOLD_MAY_NOT_BE_CONSUMED_AS_RELEASED
    - RAW_FAILED_MAY_NOT_BE_CONSUMED_AS_HOLD
    - GATE_EVENT_NEQ_GATE_RETURN
    - GATE_RETURN_NEQ_DECISION_RECEIPT
    - RECEIPT_MAY_NOT_CREATE_GATE_EXECUTION
```

---

# L5.1-18｜Final Release

```yaml
FINAL_RELEASE_V5_1:

  owner:
    LIHUO_LIGHTER

  allowed_only_if:
    - FINAL_GATE_EXECUTED
    - FINAL_GATE_DECISION_RELEASED
    - MAIN_COVERAGE_CLOSED_FOR_CURRENT_SNAPSHOT
    - RELEASE_CONTRACT_SATISFIED

  final_release_receipt:
    required_when_released: true

  may_not_be_created_by:
    - DIRECTOR
    - MAIN
    - VERTICAL_OWNER
    - LEDGER_PROJECTION
    - VISIBLE_RECEIPT_INDEX
```

```text
MAIN SOLVES.
LIGHTER RELEASES.
```

不變。

---

# L5.1-19｜Visible Receipt Index

```text
EVERY USER-VISIBLE LIHUO-GOVERNED RESPONSE
MUST CARRY
THE VISIBLE RECEIPT INDEX
AS THE FINAL VISIBLE SEGMENT
OF THE HUMAN-FACING MANIFESTATION ENVELOPE.
```

V5.1：

```yaml
VISIBLE_RECEIPT_INDEX_V5_1:

  source:
    - GOVERNING_CYCLE_ACCOUNTABILITY_LEDGER_V5_1

  required_trace_anchor:
    - GOVERNING_CYCLE_REF
    - GOVERNING_CYCLE_CLASS
    - ACCOUNTABILITY_LEDGER_REF

  native_mode_may_project:
    - NATIVE_CYCLE_ID_ALIAS

  may_project:
    - GENERATION_ATTEMPT_STATE
    - GOVERNANCE_ADMISSION_STATE
    - PRIMARY_GOVERNING_CYCLE_STANDING
    - PRIMARY_STANDING_QUALIFICATION_REF
    - CYCLE_CONTINUITY_STATE_IF_VERTICAL
    - CYCLE_CONTINUITY_QUALIFICATION_REF_IF_VERTICAL
    - R1_STATE
    - O9_STATE
    - I9_BRANCH_STATE
    - R2_STATE
    - MAIN_RETURN_STATE
    - MAIN_COVERAGE_STATE
    - RADAR_OCCURRENCE_STATE
    - FINAL_GATE_EXECUTION_STATE
    - FINAL_GATE_DECISION_IF_EXECUTED
    - FINAL_RELEASE_RECEIPT_REF_IF_PRESENT

  may_not:
    - FORCE_PARALLEL_LIHUO_CYCLE_ID
    - INVENT_RECEIPTS
    - INVENT_OWNER_EXECUTION
    - EXPLAIN_HIDDEN_COT
```

Pre-primary：

```yaml
PRE_GOVERNING_CYCLE_STATUS_INDEX_V5_1:

  attempt_ref: REF_OR_NONE
  attempt_state:
    - NOT_ESTABLISHED
    - ATTEMPT_PENDING_GOVERNING_CYCLE

  governing_cycle_ref: NONE
  accountability_ledger_ref: NONE
  prior_accountability_ledger_ref: REF_OR_NONE

  may_project:
    - ADMISSION_STATUS
    - PRE_GOVERNING_CYCLE_GAP_REF
```

---


# L5.1-19A｜Coverage State Canonical Projection

```yaml
COVERAGE_STATE_PROJECTION:

  MAIN_COVERAGE_PRECONDITIONS_READY:
    lighter_ledger:
      preconditions_state: READY

  MAIN_COVERAGE_CLOSED:
    lighter_ledger:
      closure_state: CLOSED_FOR_SNAPSHOT
```

```text
PRECONDITIONS_SATISFIED_FOR_CURRENT_SNAPSHOT
!=
COVERAGE_CLOSED_FOR_CURRENT_SNAPSHOT.
```

# L5.1-20｜Legacy / Auxiliary Cycle Containment

```yaml
AUXILIARY_CYCLE_COMPATIBILITY:

  allowed:
    - DOMAIN_BOOKKEEPING
    - LEGACY_BINDING_REFERENCE
    - SUBORDINATE_REVIEW_CONTAINER

  may_not:
    - BECOME_SECOND_ACTIVE_PRIMARY
    - PROVIDE_MAIN_COVERAGE_FOR_PRIMARY
    - PROVIDE_FINAL_GATE_FOR_PRIMARY
    - PROVIDE_FINAL_RELEASE_FOR_PRIMARY
    - RETROACTIVELY_CURE_PRIMARY_FAILURE
```

---

# L5.1-20A｜Stack Current Index / Version Resolution Preservation

Current Lighter 的 version-resolution responsibility 與 Cycle identity ownership 無關，V5.1 完整保留。

```yaml
LIGHTER_VERSION_RESOLUTION_V5_1:

  consumes:
    - QUALIFIED_LIHUO_STACK_CURRENT_INDEX

  may_resolve:
    - PROTOCOL_PEER
    - LIGHTER_PEER
    - MAIN_PEER
    - DECLARED_COMPATIBLE_SUPPLEMENTS

  may_not:
    - SELF_ELECT_PROTOCOL_VERSION
    - SELF_ELECT_LIGHTER_VERSION
    - SELF_ELECT_MAIN_VERSION
    - SORT_REPOSITORY_AND_CHOOSE_LATEST
    - TREAT_DRAFT_AS_CURRENT_WITHOUT_AUTHORITY
```

硬鎖：

```text
LIGHTER CONSUMES
THE QUALIFIED CURRENT STACK INDEX.

LIGHTER MAY NOT
SELF-ELECT PROTOCOL / LIGHTER / MAIN PEERS.
```

---

# L5.1-21｜Coding Boundary

```yaml
LIGHTER_V5_1_CODING_BOUNDARY:

  code_may:
    - VALIDATE_ATTEMPT_ORIGIN_SCHEMA
    - VALIDATE_GOVERNING_CYCLE_IDENTITY_PROVENANCE
    - INSTANTIATE_NATIVE_GOVERNING_CYCLE_CONTAINER_AFTER_LAWFUL_ESTABLISHMENT
    - OPEN_GOVERNANCE_TICKET
    - OPEN_ACCOUNTABILITY_LEDGER
    - RECORD_QUALIFIED_GAP_REF
    - APPEND_ACTUAL_RECEIPT_REF
    - TRANSPORT_PREBOUND_REQUESTS
    - CAPTURE_RAW_RETURNS
    - VALIDATE_REQUEST_RETURN_HASHES
    - PROJECT_LEDGER
    - PROJECT_VISIBLE_INDEX
    - VALIDATE_PHASE_TRANSITION_AGAINST_DAIL_DECLARED_RULES
    - HASH_QUALIFIED_INPUT_PACKAGE
    - VALIDATE_QIP_TO_MAIN_EXACT_CONTEXT_BINDING
    - INSTANTIATE_AND_SERIALIZE_PREQUALIFIED_MAIN_INVOCATION_AUTHORITY_BINDING
    - VALIDATE_PRIMARY_STANDING_RECORD_SCHEMA_REF_AND_SNAPSHOT_BINDING
    - VALIDATE_CONTINUITY_EVIDENCE_SCHEMA_AND_PROVENANCE_BINDING
    - RECORD_CYCLE_CONTINUITY_STATE
    - PROPAGATE_QUALIFIED_CONTINUITY_DIRTY_STALE_FLAGS
    - RECORD_QUALIFIED_TERMINATION_EFFECT
    - RECORD_PREQUALIFIED_LEDGER_CLOSURE_TRANSITION

  code_may_not:
    - SELF_MINT_VERTICAL_CYCLE_AUTHORITY
    - SELF_MINT_ATTEMPT_TERMINATION
    - DECIDE_MAIN_OPTIONALITY
    - INVENT_MAIN_RETURN
    - INVENT_GATE_RETURN
    - INVENT_HOLD_WHEN_GATE_DID_NOT_RUN
    - TURN_HOLD_INTO_ATTEMPT_TERMINATION
    - TURN_FAILED_GATE_INTO_ATTEMPT_TERMINATION
    - DECLARE_COVERAGE_CLOSED_WITH_ZERO_MAIN
    - DECIDE_RELEASE_FROM_RECEIPT_SHAPE_ALONE
    - REOPEN_CLOSED_ATTEMPT_WITHOUT_SUCCESSOR_AUTHORITY
    - TREAT_EXTERNAL_CYCLE_CLOSE_AS_AUTOMATIC_RELEASE
    - TREAT_INITIAL_VERTICAL_ADMISSION_AS_PERMANENT
    - RESTORE_OLD_QIP_FROM_CONTINUITY_REFRESH_ALONE
    - RESTORE_OLD_MAIN_COVERAGE_FROM_CONTINUITY_REFRESH_ALONE
    - KEEP_REVOKED_PRIMARY_ACTIVE_TO_AVOID_ZERO_PRIMARY
    - SELF_ELECT_PROTOCOL_LIGHTER_MAIN_PEERS
    - QUALIFY_CALLER_AUTHORITY_OR_INVOCATION_STANDING
    - DECIDE_OR_UPGRADE_PRIMARY_STANDING
    - QUALIFY_CONTINUITY_STATE_FROM_RAW_EVIDENCE
    - DECIDE_MATERIAL_SCOPE_OR_AUTHORITY_EFFECT
    - TURN_ENUM_OR_FIELD_PRESENCE_INTO_GOVERNANCE_DECISION
    - TREAT_HASH_MATCH_AS_SEMANTIC_QUALIFICATION
    - COLLAPSE_UNKNOWN_PARTIAL_OR_UNEVIDENCED_TO_FALSE_FAILED_OR_HOLD
```

---


Coding interpretation lock：

```text
VALIDATE
=
SCHEMA / REF / HASH / SNAPSHOT / DECLARED-TRANSITION CONSISTENCY CHECK.

VALIDATE
!=
SEMANTIC QUALIFICATION.
```

```text
INSTANTIATE / SERIALIZE PREQUALIFIED BINDING
!=
CREATE AUTHORITY.

RECORD QUALIFIED STATE
!=
DECIDE THE STATE.
```

所有 `CURRENT / DIRTY / STALE / HOLD / FAILED / CLOSED / READY` 等欄位，
若屬治理法位，必須先有其 lawful owner / qualification basis；
Coding 只能承載、驗形、接線、投影，不得從欄位存在性反推法位。

# L5.1-22｜Inherited Root Supersession × Preservation

只有與 V5.1 明示衝突的 Cycle / accountability 語言被 supersede。

其餘非衝突 Current Lighter control-plane authority：

```text
RETAINED.
```

包括但不限於：

```text
SELF-STANDING GATE
LLIA / PREMISE STANDING
ATTENTION-EFFECT QUALIFICATION
EXTERNAL-CONSTRAINT SEPARATION
COMPETITION INTEGRITY
OUTPUT CONTRACT / SUFFICIENCY
CAPABILITY ELIGIBILITY
FUNCTIONAL MODULE ORCHESTRATION
PROVIDER / TOOL / TRANSPORT GOVERNANCE
EVIDENCE ADMISSION
RECOVERY / REAUTHORIZATION
VERSION RESOLUTION
```

以下 Current / inherited 語言在 V5.1 successor 中明確 supersede：

```text
V5.0.x:
EVERY ESTABLISHED LIHUO CYCLE
IS CREATED BY LIGHTER
WITH cycle_id / ticket / manifest / ledger.

→

V5.1.0:
LIGHTER CREATES THE PRIMARY CYCLE IDENTITY
ONLY IN NATIVE MODE.

IN AUTHORIZED VERTICAL MODE,
LIGHTER CONSUMES THE LAWFUL PRIMARY CYCLE IDENTITY
AND CREATES ONLY LIHUO GOVERNANCE OBJECTS.
```

以及：

```text
V5.0.x:
EVERY LIHUO CYCLE TRANSITS THROUGH MAIN.

→

V5.1.0:
EVERY ESTABLISHED LIHUO-GOVERNED
GENERATIVE PRIMARY CYCLE
MUST ACHIEVE MAIN COVERAGE.

MAIN COVERAGE
REQUIRES
AT LEAST ONE LAWFUL MAIN TRANSIT.
```

以及：

```text
V5.0.x:
VISIBLE INDEX REQUIRES cycle_id + ledger_ref.

→

V5.1.0:
VISIBLE INDEX REQUIRES
governing_cycle_ref
+
governing_cycle_class
+
accountability_ledger_ref.
```

---

# L5.1-23｜Non-Auto-Rebind / Host Boundary

```text
LIGHTER V5.1.0 RATIFIED
!=
EXISTING VERTICAL AUTO-REBOUND.

LIGHTER V5.1.0 DOCUMENT
!=
HOST OWNER SURFACE DEPLOYED.
```

Existing Verticals 仍須：

```text
COMPATIBILITY REVIEW
→ EXPLICIT POINTER REBIND
→ MATERIAL ASSENT WHEN REQUIRED.
```

本稿不修改 Guanzhi。

# 25｜RC2.1 最短口訣

```text
PROTOCOL GOVERNS.

ATTEMPT IDENTITY MUST HAVE AN AUTHORIZED ORIGIN.

ONE ESTABLISHED LIHUO-GOVERNED ATTEMPT
HAS ONE ACTIVE PRIMARY GOVERNING CYCLE.

LIGHTER MAY CREATE THE PRIMARY
IN NATIVE MODE.

LIGHTER MAY GOVERN A PRIMARY
IT DID NOT CREATE
IN VERTICAL MODE.

LIGHTER ADMITS.
LLIA QUALIFIES PREMISES.
MAIN COMPUTES.
LIGHTER QUALIFIES DIRTY / STALE / REENTRY.

ZERO MAIN
CAN NEVER
SATISFY MAIN COVERAGE.

GATE CONSUMES
THE LATEST COVERING MAIN RETURN.

HOLD
IS NOT
ATTEMPT DEATH.

EXTERNAL CYCLE STANDING
MUST REMAIN CURRENT
FOR LIHUO ADMISSION TO REMAIN CURRENT.

CONTINUITY CHANGE
PROPAGATES TO
QIP AND MAIN COVERAGE.

IF THE PRIMARY REALLY DISAPPEARS,
THE ATTEMPT RETURNS TO PENDING.
DO NOT PRETEND A DEAD PRIMARY IS ACTIVE.

CYCLE IDENTITY DECOUPLING
DOES NOT TRANSFER
THE REST OF THE LIGHTER CONTROL PLANE.

VISIBLE RECEIPTS
FOLLOW THE PRIMARY GOVERNING CYCLE.

LIGHTER RELEASES.
```

# L5-0｜Root

```text
RELATION FIRST.
CONTRACT SECOND.
MODULE THIRD.
CODING AFTERWARD.

DAIL DEFINES.
IR NORMALIZES.
CODING CONNECTS.
RUNTIME EXECUTES.

CODING MAY SCHEDULE.
CODING MAY NOT SOLVE.
```

# L5-1｜Cycle Open Objects

每個 established LIHUO cycle 由 Lighter 建立：

```yaml
LIHUO_CYCLE_OPEN:

  cycle_id: REQUIRED
  ticket_ref: REQUIRED
  manifest_ref: REQUIRED
  ledger_ref: REQUIRED
  scope_ref: REQUIRED
  opened_snapshot_ref: REQUIRED

  does_not_prove:
    - MAIN_EXECUTED
    - RADAR_EXECUTED
    - FINAL_GATE_EXECUTED
    - FINAL_RELEASE
```

# L5-2｜Cycle Topology

```text
ESTABLISHED LIHUO CYCLE
├─ opens ticket / manifest / ledger
├─ Radar becomes cycle-resident parallel observer
└─ Main transit obligation becomes active

MAIN:
R1 → O9 → [I9 if O9 triggered] → R2
→ MAIN ACTUAL-STATE RETURN IF MAIN WAS REACHED

LIGHTER:
PRE_RELEASE LEDGER VIEW
→ FINAL GATE EXECUTION ATTEMPT
→ [decision only if Gate executed]
→ POST-GATE LEDGER STATE
→ Visible Receipt Index as final visible segment
```

```text
RADAR PARALLEL OBSERVER
!=
R1 PRECONDITION.

FINAL RELEASE RECEIPT
!=
FINAL GATE INPUT.
```

# L5-3｜Radar Parallel Observation

```yaml
RADAR_CYCLE_OBSERVATION:

  owner: LIGHTER.RADAR_V6

  activation:
    cycle_resident: true
    main_predecessor: false

  observation_points_may_include:
    - CYCLE_OPEN
    - POST_R1
    - POST_O9
    - POST_I9_IF_ANY
    - POST_R2
    - PRE_RELEASE

  occurrence_state:
    - ADVISORY_RETURNED
    - NO_MATERIAL_ADVICE
    - FAILED
    - MISSING
    - NOT_REACHED

  content_authority:
    binding: false
    closure_effect: NONE
    path_winner: false

  every_established_cycle:
    MUST_PRESERVE_AND_PROJECT:
      - QUALIFIED_OCCURRENCE_STATE
      - OR_QUALIFIED_TRACE_GAP

  missing_or_failed_alone:
    generic_release_veto: false
```

若 Radar execution 已啟動但失敗：

```yaml
RADAR_FAILURE_RECEIPT:
  required_when:
    - RADAR_EXECUTION_STARTED
    - FAILURE_EVENT_OCCURRED
  proves:
    - FAILURE_EVENT_ONLY
  may_not_prove:
    - NO_MATERIAL_ADVICE
```

# L5-4｜Late Radar / Reentry

```text
RADAR ADVICE
!=
MAIN REENTRY TICKET.
```

```yaml
RADAR_LATE_TIMING:

  PRE_R2:
    may_be_advisory_input_to_main: true

  POST_R2_PRE_FINAL_GATE:
    lighter_may_qualify_same_cycle_reentry: true

  DURING_FINAL_GATE_BEFORE_DECISION:
    lighter_may:
      - HOLD_GATE_EXECUTION_PENDING_REENTRY_QUALIFICATION
      - QUALIFY_REENTRY_IF_LAWFUL
    radar_may_force_reentry: false

  AFTER_FINAL_RELEASE_OR_LEDGER_CLOSED:
    same_cycle_reopen: false
    allowed:
      - OPEN_SUCCESSOR_REVIEW_CYCLE
      - OPEN_NEW_LAWFUL_CYCLE
    retroactive_rewrite: forbidden
```

# L5-5｜Required Execution Manifest

Manifest 是 obligation map，不是 execution proof。

```yaml
REQUIRED_EXECUTION_MANIFEST_V5:

  manifest_ref: REQUIRED
  cycle_id: REQUIRED
  ticket_ref: REQUIRED

  main_obligations:
    R1: REQUIRED
    O9: REQUIRED
    I9: CONDITIONAL_BY_O9
    R2: REQUIRED
    MAIN_RETURN:
      REQUIRED_IF_MAIN_EXECUTION_WAS_REACHED_ENOUGH_TO_RETURN_STATE
    CYCLE_RETURN_RECEIPT:
      REQUIRED_IF_MAIN_RETURN_EXISTS

  I9:
    if_O9_I9_NOT_TRIGGERED:
      stage_required: false
      receipt_required: false
      receipt_forbidden: true
    if_O9_I9_TRIGGERED:
      stage_required: true
      receipt_required: true

  radar:
    standing: MANDATORY_TRACE_TARGET
    generic_release_veto: false

  pre_gate_prohibited_requirements:
    - FINAL_RELEASE_DECISION_RECEIPT
    - CLOSED_LEDGER_SNAPSHOT
```

# L5-6｜Failure-Aware Cycle Receipt Ledger

```yaml
CYCLE_RECEIPT_LEDGER_V5:

  identity:
    ledger_ref: REQUIRED
    cycle_id: REQUIRED
    ticket_ref: REQUIRED
    manifest_ref: REQUIRED
    scope_ref: REQUIRED

  phase:
    - OPEN
    - PRE_RELEASE
    - POST_GATE
    - CLOSED
    - TERMINAL_GAP

  stage_state:

    R1:
      status: [PRESENT, GAP, FAILED, NOT_REACHED]
      receipt_ref: REF_OR_NONE
      gap_or_failure_ref: REF_OR_NONE

    O9:
      status: [PRESENT, GAP, FAILED, NOT_REACHED]
      receipt_ref: REF_OR_NONE
      gap_or_failure_ref: REF_OR_NONE

    I9:
      branch_state: [TRIGGERED, NOT_TRIGGERED, UNKNOWN, NOT_REACHED]
      receipt_ref: REF_IF_TRIGGERED_AND_PRESENT_ELSE_NONE
      gap_or_failure_ref: REF_OR_NONE

    R2:
      status: [PRESENT, GAP, FAILED, NOT_REACHED]
      receipt_ref: REF_OR_NONE
      gap_or_failure_ref: REF_OR_NONE

    MAIN_RETURN:
      status:
        - PRESENT_COMPLETE
        - PRESENT_PARTIAL
        - PRESENT_FAILED_STATE
        - MISSING
        - NOT_REACHED
      return_ref: REF_OR_NONE
      cycle_return_receipt_ref: REF_OR_NONE
      gap_ref: REF_OR_NONE

  radar:
    occurrence_state:
      - ADVISORY_RETURNED
      - NO_MATERIAL_ADVICE
      - FAILED
      - MISSING
      - NOT_REACHED
    evidence_ref: REF_OR_NONE

  final_gate:
    execution_state:
      - NOT_REACHED
      - EXECUTED
      - UNAVAILABLE
      - UNEVIDENCED
    decision:
      - NONE
      - RELEASED
      - HOLD
      - FAILED
    decision_receipt_ref: REF_OR_NONE

  locks:
    - GAP_STATE_MUST_HAVE_QUALIFIED_BASIS
    - LEDGER_MAY_NOT_IMPERSONATE_OWNER_RETURN
    - NO_GATE_EXECUTION_NEQ_GATE_HOLD
```

# L5-7｜Pre-Release View

Final Gate 讀：

```yaml
PRE_RELEASE_LEDGER_VIEW:

  requires:
    - CYCLE_ID
    - EXPECTED_CONTROL_FLOW_OBLIGATIONS
    - CURRENT_STAGE_STATES
    - AVAILABLE_RECEIPT_REFS
    - AVAILABLE_OWNER_RETURN_REFS
    - QUALIFIED_GAP_REFS
    - CURRENT_SCOPE_AND_STANDING

  does_not_require:
    - ALL_RECEIPTS_PRESENT
    - FINAL_RELEASE_DECISION_RECEIPT
    - CLOSED_LEDGER_SNAPSHOT
```

# L5-8｜Final Gate Admission

```text
GATE JUDGES MISSING EVIDENCE.
MISSING EVIDENCE DOES NOT ERASE THE GATE.
```

```yaml
LIHUO_FINAL_GATE_V5:

  admission_requires:
    - QUALIFIED_PRE_RELEASE_LEDGER_VIEW
    - CURRENT_SCOPE_AND_STANDING

  consume_if_present:
    - R1_RECEIPT
    - O9_RECEIPT
    - I9_RECEIPT_IF_TRIGGERED
    - R2_RECEIPT
    - MAIN_RETURN
    - CYCLE_RETURN_RECEIPT
    - RADAR_EVIDENCE

  qualified_gap_states:
    - INPUT_TO_GATE_JUDGMENT

  missing_receipt_alone:
    blocks_gate_execution: false

  decision_owner:
    LIHUO_LIGHTER
```

# L5-9｜Final Gate Execution State / Decision

```yaml
FINAL_GATE_EXECUTION_ATTEMPT:

  EXECUTED:
    decision:
      - RELEASED
      - HOLD
      - FAILED
    final_release_decision_receipt: REQUIRED

  NOT_REACHED:
    decision: NONE
    final_release_decision_receipt: NONE

  UNAVAILABLE:
    decision: NONE
    final_release_decision_receipt: NONE

  UNEVIDENCED:
    decision: NONE
    final_release_decision_receipt: NONE
```

```text
NO FINAL-GATE EXECUTION
!=
FINAL-GATE HOLD DECISION.
```

# L5-10｜Main Return Consumption

```yaml
MAIN_RETURN_CONSUMPTION:

  acceptable:
    - COMPLETE
    - PARTIAL
    - FAILED_STATE

  main_return_present:
    consume_actual_state: true

  main_was_reached_but_return_missing:
    ledger_gap: MAIN_RETURN_MISSING

  main_never_reached:
    ledger_state: NOT_REACHED
    fabricate_main_return: false
```

# L5-11｜Cycle Return Receipt

`CYCLE_RETURN_RECEIPT` 保留，但只在 Main Return 真實存在時存在。

```text
MAIN RETURN EXISTS
→ CYCLE_RETURN_RECEIPT REQUIRED.

MAIN NEVER REACHED
→ NO MAIN RETURN
→ NO CYCLE_RETURN_RECEIPT.
```

# L5-12｜Visible Receipt Index：每輪最後一段

```text
EVERY USER-VISIBLE LIHUO-GOVERNED RESPONSE
MUST CARRY
THE VISIBLE RECEIPT INDEX
AS THE FINAL VISIBLE SEGMENT
OF THE HUMAN-FACING MANIFESTATION ENVELOPE.
```

Exact payload boundary：

```text
VISIBLE RECEIPT INDEX
ATTACHES TO
THE HUMAN-FACING MANIFESTATION ENVELOPE.

IT MUST NOT MUTATE
AN EXACT MACHINE / TOOL / SIGNED / HASH-BOUND PAYLOAD.

WHEN THE GOVERNED PAYLOAD ITSELF
MUST REMAIN BYTE-EXACT OR SCHEMA-EXACT,
THE ENCLOSING USER-VISIBLE RESPONSE
OR DESIGNATED HUMAN-FACING METADATA ENVELOPE
CARRIES THE INDEX.
```

# V4.12.2-0｜修正的是週期完整判定，不是補更多階段

V4.12.1 的錯誤不是「I9 收據還不夠多」。

真正錯誤是：

```text
I9 原本是 conditional branch
↓
被表示成 mandatory assessment stage
↓
I9 未被啟動時沒有 receipt
↓
被誤判為 missing required receipt
↓
合法週期被判 incomplete / HOLD
```

本版不再用額外 mandatory stage 修這個問題。

本版直接從**週期收據合法完成的定義**修正。

---

# V4.12.2-1｜Cycle Receipt Legal Completion｜週期收據合法完成定義

正式定義：

> **週期收據合法完成，不要求所有可能模組都有收據。**
>
> **合法完成的判定標準，是收據集合必須與本輪實際合法控制流一致：所有固定必跑模組，以及所有實際被合法啟動的條件模組，都具有對應收據；未被啟動的條件模組，不得為填滿欄位而製造該模組收據。**

最短式：

```text
CYCLE COMPLETENESS
!=
ALL POSSIBLE MODULE RECEIPTS PRESENT

CYCLE COMPLETENESS
=
RECEIPTS MATCH THE ACTUAL LAWFUL CONTROL FLOW
```

一般規則：

```text
REQUIRED MODULE
→ RECEIPT REQUIRED

CONDITIONAL MODULE TRIGGERED
→ RECEIPT REQUIRED

CONDITIONAL MODULE NOT TRIGGERED
→ RECEIPT MUST NOT EXIST
```

因此：

> **Receipt 記錄實際發生的治理步驟；Receipt 不得為未發生的治理步驟創造 execution。**

---

# V4.12.2-2｜O9 / I9 Current Control Flow

現行週期拓撲：

```text
R1
→ O9
   ├─ I9_NOT_TRIGGERED
   │  → R2
   │
   └─ I9_TRIGGERED
      → I9
      → R2
```

其中：

```text
R1 = REQUIRED
O9 = REQUIRED
I9 = CONDITIONAL_BY_O9
R2 = REQUIRED
```

O9 必須在自己的 receipt 中留下本輪 I9 activation decision：

```yaml
O9_I9_ACTIVATION_DECISION:
  decision:
    - I9_TRIGGERED
    - I9_NOT_TRIGGERED
    - HOLD_NO_VALID_DECISION
  reason_ref: REQUIRED
```

只有前兩種可以形成正常 completed branch。

`HOLD_NO_VALID_DECISION` 表示 O9 本身沒有形成可依賴的 I9 啟動判斷；不得靠「沒有 I9 receipt」自行推回 `NOT_TRIGGERED`。

---

# V4.12.2-3｜I9 Receipt Rule｜條件必備，不是 Optional

I9 Receipt 不使用「有也可以、沒有也可以」的 optional 語義。

它是**條件必備**：

```text
O9 = I9_TRIGGERED
→ I9 RECEIPT REQUIRED

O9 = I9_NOT_TRIGGERED
→ I9 RECEIPT MUST NOT EXIST
```

因此：

```text
O9 = I9_TRIGGERED
+
I9 RECEIPT MISSING
=
CYCLE INCOMPLETE / HOLD
```

```text
O9 = I9_NOT_TRIGGERED
+
NO I9 RECEIPT
=
LEGAL COMPLETE BRANCH
```

```text
O9 = I9_NOT_TRIGGERED
+
I9 RECEIPT EXISTS
=
CONTROL_FLOW_RECEIPT_MISMATCH / HOLD
```

`I9_NOT_TRIGGERED`：

```text
!= I9_FAILED
!= I9_NOT_QUALIFIED
!= MISSING_I9
```

它只表示：

> **本輪 O9 沒有啟動 I9，因此 I9 不進入本輪 receipt obligation。**

---

# V4.12.2-4｜I9 被啟動後的收據

本版不取消既有 I9 qualification、scope、evidence 與 execution 邊界。

只改一件事：**這些 I9 內部判定只在 O9 已合法啟動 I9 後才進場。**

因此 I9 被啟動後，必須形成一張可追蹤的 I9 stage receipt；該 receipt 可以記錄：

```yaml
I9_RECEIPT:
  i9_receipt_ref: REQUIRED_IF_O9_TRIGGERED
  cycle_id: REQUIRED
  trigger_o9_receipt_ref: REQUIRED
  trigger_state: I9_TRIGGERED

  qualification_result:
    - QUALIFIED
    - NOT_QUALIFIED
    - EVIDENCE_GAP
    - LAMP_REQUIRED_MISSING

  execution_state:
    - EXECUTED
    - NOT_EXECUTED

  execution_ref: REF_OR_NULL_WITH_REASON
```

所以：

```text
I9 TRIGGERED
!=
I9 MUST EXECUTE
```

但：

```text
I9 TRIGGERED
=
I9 RECEIPT MUST EXIST
```

I9 Receipt 可以合法記錄「被啟動後未取得執行資格」；但不得在 O9 未啟動 I9 時憑空存在。

---

# V4.12.2-5｜Current Cycle Execution Manifest

現行有效 Manifest 語義改為：

```yaml
REQUIRED_EXECUTION_MANIFEST:
  manifest_ref: REQUIRED
  cycle_id: REQUIRED
  ticket_ref: REQUIRED

  required_modules:
    - MAIN.RCCC_R1
    - MAIN.SAC_O9
    - MAIN.RCCC_R2

  conditional_modules:
    MAIN.SAC_I9:
      trigger_owner: MAIN.SAC_O9
      activation_evidence: O9_RECEIPT.I9_ACTIVATION_DECISION
      receipt_rule:
        if_triggered: REQUIRED
        if_not_triggered: PROHIBITED

    MAIN.RCCC_R3_PLUS:
      activation: EVENT_DRIVEN

    OTHER_MAIN_EVENT_DRIVEN_MODULES:
      activation: EVENT_DRIVEN

  required_receipt_classes:
    - R1_EXECUTION_RECEIPT
    - O9_EXECUTION_RECEIPT
    - R2_EXECUTION_RECEIPT
    - CYCLE_RETURN_RECEIPT

  conditional_receipt_classes:
    I9_RECEIPT:
      required_when: O9_I9_ACTIVATION_DECISION == I9_TRIGGERED
      prohibited_when: O9_I9_ACTIVATION_DECISION == I9_NOT_TRIGGERED

  visibility_default: SILENT_BACKEND
```

Manifest 仍然只是「本輪控制流需要什麼證據」的規格，不是 execution proof。

---

# V4.12.2-6｜Current Cycle Receipt Ledger

現行有效 Ledger 語義改為：

```yaml
CYCLE_RECEIPT_LEDGER:
  ledger_ref: REQUIRED
  cycle_id: REQUIRED
  ticket_ref: REQUIRED
  manifest_ref: REQUIRED
  scope_ref: REQUIRED
  opened_snapshot_ref: REQUIRED
  closed_snapshot_ref: REQUIRED_OR_HOLD

  module_receipts:
    R1: REQUIRED_REF
    O9: REQUIRED_REF
    I9: CONDITIONAL_REF_BY_O9_ACTIVATION
    R2: REQUIRED_REF
    R3_PLUS: REF_LIST_IF_TRIGGERED

  control_flow_state:
    i9_activation_source: O9_RECEIPT
    i9_activation:
      - I9_TRIGGERED
      - I9_NOT_TRIGGERED
      - HOLD_NO_VALID_DECISION

  evidence_origin_summary: REQUIRED
  missing_required_receipts: []
  unexpected_receipts: []
  unresolved_evidence_gaps: []

  cycle_execution_state:
    - COMPLETE
    - INCOMPLETE
    - HOLD
    - FAILED
```

判定順序：

```text
FIRST:
READ O9 I9-ACTIVATION DECISION

THEN:
DETERMINE WHETHER I9 RECEIPT IS REQUIRED OR PROHIBITED

THEN:
CHECK RECEIPT MATCH
```

禁止使用：

```text
I9 receipt field empty
→ therefore missing
```

必須先問：

```text
WAS I9 TRIGGERED?
```

---

# V4.12.2-7｜Final Gate Receipt Admission

LIGHTER Final Gate 現行判定：

```text
missing mandatory R1 receipt
→ HOLD

missing mandatory O9 receipt
→ HOLD / EVIDENCE GAP

O9 has no valid I9 activation decision
→ HOLD

O9 = I9_NOT_TRIGGERED
+
I9 receipt absent
→ LEGAL

O9 = I9_NOT_TRIGGERED
+
I9 receipt present
→ CONTROL_FLOW_RECEIPT_MISMATCH / HOLD

O9 = I9_TRIGGERED
+
I9 receipt absent
→ HOLD / MISSING_TRIGGERED_MODULE_RECEIPT

O9 = I9_TRIGGERED
+
I9 receipt present
→ CONTINUE RECEIPT ADMISSION

missing mandatory R2 receipt
→ HOLD / NO RELEASE
```

Final Gate 不得：

```text
把未觸發 I9 的 receipt 缺席判成 missing receipt
```

也不得：

```text
為了讓欄位完整而補造 I9 receipt
```

---

# V4.12.2-8｜LIGHTER ↔ MAIN Receipt Contract 小修正

現行 LIGHTER 對 Main return 的 receipt expectation：

```yaml
MAIN_TO_LIGHTER_RETURN:
  main_cycle_return_ref: REQUIRED
  cycle_receipt_ledger_ref: REQUIRED
  r1_receipt_ref: REQUIRED
  o9_receipt_ref: REQUIRED

  i9_receipt_ref:
    required_if: O9_I9_ACTIVATION_DECISION == I9_TRIGGERED
    prohibited_if: O9_I9_ACTIVATION_DECISION == I9_NOT_TRIGGERED

  r2_receipt_ref: REQUIRED
  r3_plus_receipt_refs: IF_TRIGGERED
  unresolved_evidence_gap_refs: []
  failure_receipt_refs: []
```

LIGHTER 不重新計算 Main；LIGHTER 只核對：

```text
receipt standing
+
receipt/control-flow match
+
dependency coherence
+
scope coherence
+
freshness
+
final release eligibility
```

---

# V4.12.2-9｜Light Trace 同步規則

Light Trace 仍然只顯影已存在的 receipt，不製造 receipt。

現行資料源：

```text
R1 receipt
O9 receipt
I9 receipt IF O9 TRIGGERED
R2 receipt
Final Gate receipt
```

若：

```text
O9 = I9_NOT_TRIGGERED
```

則 Light Trace 應顯示 O9 的 `I9_NOT_TRIGGERED` 決定；**不得另外生成一張 I9「未執行證明」來補版面。**

---

# V4.12.2-10｜Supersession Map｜只撤錯誤控制語義，不洗歷史

V4.12.1 全文保留於附錄 A，不回溯修改。

但下列 V4.12.1 現行語義自本版起失效：

```text
I9 ASSESSMENT = MANDATORY IN EVERY ESTABLISHED CYCLE

NO I9 ASSESSMENT RECEIPT
→ ALWAYS CYCLE INCOMPLETE

O9 PASS
→ MUST STILL CREATE A SEPARATE I9 ASSESSMENT RECEIPT
```

同時，父系中任何與本版衝突的：

```text
I9_ASSESSMENT: REQUIRED_REF
I9_ASSESSMENT_RECEIPT: REQUIRED IN ALL CYCLES
CONDITIONAL MODULE NOT EXECUTED → MUST STILL HAVE I9 ASSESSMENT RECEIPT
```

均保留其歷史形成位置，但不再作為 Current Lighter 的 operative reading。

未與本版衝突的內容全部繼承，包括：

```text
NO POST-HOC RECEIPT MINTING
RECEIPT RECORDS WHAT ACTUALLY HAPPENED
FORMAL != REFERENCE
EVIDENCE ORIGIN CLASSIFICATION
I9 QUALIFICATION / SCOPE / HARD-BOUNDARY LIMITS
R1 / O9 / R2 FIXED OBLIGATIONS
R3+ EVENT-DRIVEN PRINCIPLE
FINAL GATE MAY NOT INVENT RECEIPTS
```

---

# V4.12.2-11｜Regression Tests

```yaml
CYCLE_RECEIPT_CONTROL_FLOW_REGRESSION_V0_1:

  CRCF_01_I9_NOT_TRIGGERED_NO_RECEIPT:
    input:
      - O9_RECEIPT_PRESENT
      - O9_I9_ACTIVATION = I9_NOT_TRIGGERED

    expected:

  CRCF_02_I9_TRIGGERED_RECEIPT_MISSING:
    input:
      - O9_I9_ACTIVATION = I9_TRIGGERED
      - I9_RECEIPT_ABSENT
    expected:

      - MISSING_TRIGGERED_MODULE_RECEIPT

  CRCF_03_I9_NOT_TRIGGERED_RECEIPT_PRESENT:
    input:
      - O9_I9_ACTIVATION = I9_NOT_TRIGGERED
      - I9_RECEIPT_PRESENT
    expected:

      - CONTROL_FLOW_RECEIPT_MISMATCH

  CRCF_04_I9_TRIGGERED_NOT_QUALIFIED:
    input:
      - O9_I9_ACTIVATION = I9_TRIGGERED
      - I9_RECEIPT_PRESENT
      - I9_QUALIFICATION = NOT_QUALIFIED
      - I9_EXECUTION = NOT_EXECUTED
    expected:
      - LEGAL_I9_STAGE_COMPLETION
      - CONTINUE_TO_R2

  CRCF_05_NO_O9_ACTIVATION_DECISION:
    input:
      - O9_RECEIPT_PRESENT
      - I9_ACTIVATION_DECISION_MISSING
    expected:
      - HOLD
      - DO_NOT_INFER_NOT_TRIGGERED_FROM_ABSENCE_OF_I9_RECEIPT

  CRCF_06_NO_POST_HOC_FILL:
    input:
```

## I9 Assessment Short-Circuit Patch
### O9 PASS 不得省略 I9 Assessment｜小改版正式批准｜2026-10-12

> **法位：** `DOCUMENT_LEVEL_RATIFIED_SUCCESSOR / CURRENT_LIHUO_LIGHTER / NORMATIVE_WITHIN_LIHUO_RUNTIME_SPEC / NOT_HOST_DEPLOYED`。

> 本版正式鎖定：
>
> **O9 PASS / CLEAN / NO DISTORTION FOUND ≠ I9 ASSESSMENT WAIVER。**
>
> **I9 Execution 可以不發生；I9 Assessment 不得因此消失。**

# V4.12.1-0｜這次抓到的是 Assessment 被短路

失效鏈：

```text
O9
↓
「看起來沒有需要調燈」
↓
模型推論：「那 I9 不需要」
↓
I9 ASSESSMENT 被一起省略
↓
沒有 I9 ASSESSMENT RECEIPT
↓
週期看似完整，但強制證據鏈已斷
```

本版命名：

# `I9_ASSESSMENT_SHORT_CIRCUIT`

中文：**I9 資格審查短路**。

---

# V4.12.1-1｜四個必須分開的狀態

```text
O9 OBSERVATION RESULT
!=
I9 ASSESSMENT RESULT

I9 ASSESSMENT RESULT
!=
I9 EXECUTION

I9 EXECUTION ABSENT
!=
I9 ASSESSMENT ABSENT

I9 ASSESSMENT ABSENT
!=
LEGAL "NO ACTION NEEDED"
```

尤其：

```text
NO INTERVENTION REQUIRED
!=
NO ASSESSMENT REQUIRED
```

---

# V4.12.1-2｜Current I9 Cycle Lock

對每個已成立的正式 LIHUO cycle：

```text
R1
→ O9
→ I9 ASSESSMENT
→ R2
```

其中：

```text
I9 ASSESSMENT = MANDATORY
I9 EXECUTION = CONDITIONAL
```

合法分支：

```text
O9
↓
I9 ASSESSMENT
↓
├─ QUALIFIED
│  → I9 EXECUTION
│  → I9 EXECUTION RECEIPT
│
├─ NOT_QUALIFIED
│  → NO I9 EXECUTION
│  → I9 ASSESSMENT RECEIPT REQUIRED
│
├─ EVIDENCE_GAP
│  → NO I9 EXECUTION
│  → I9 ASSESSMENT RECEIPT REQUIRED
│
└─ LAMP_REQUIRED_MISSING
   → NO I9 EXECUTION
   → I9 ASSESSMENT RECEIPT REQUIRED
```

禁止：

```text
O9 PASS
→ SKIP I9 ASSESSMENT
```

---

# V4.12.1-3｜O9 PASS 不產生豁免權

`O9 PASS`、`O9 CLEAN`、`NO MATERIAL DISTORTION FOUND` 等語句若存在，其法位只能表示：

> **當前 O9 觀測沒有找到足以支持 I9 intervention 的明顯條件。**

它不得建立：

```text
ASSESSMENT_NOT_REQUIRED
```

更不得建立：

```text
ASSESSMENT_RECEIPT_NOT_REQUIRED
```

所以：

```text
O9 PASS
MAY SUPPORT
I9_ASSESSMENT_RESULT = NOT_QUALIFIED

BUT:

O9 PASS
MAY NOT REPLACE
I9 ASSESSMENT
```

---

# V4.12.1-4｜Receipt Rule 小修正

```yaml
I9_ASSESSMENT_RECEIPT_RULE_V0_1:

  mandatory_when:
    - ESTABLISHED_LIHUO_CYCLE

  required_even_if:
    - O9_PASS
    - O9_CLEAN
    - NO_DISTORTION_FOUND
    - I9_EXECUTION_NOT_REQUIRED
    - I9_EXECUTION_NOT_QUALIFIED

  allowed_assessment_outcomes:
    - QUALIFIED
    - NOT_QUALIFIED
    - EVIDENCE_GAP
    - LAMP_REQUIRED_MISSING

  illegal_shortcuts:
    - O9_RESULT_USED_AS_ASSESSMENT_RECEIPT
    - ABSENT_I9_EXECUTION_TREATED_AS_PROOF_OF_ASSESSMENT
    - MODEL_SELF_ASSERTS_NO_I9_NEEDED
    - FINAL_GATE_INFERS_MISSING_ASSESSMENT_FROM_SMOOTH_OUTPUT
```

硬鎖：

```text
NO I9 EXECUTION
REQUIRES
A VALID I9 ASSESSMENT OUTCOME

NO I9 ASSESSMENT RECEIPT
→
CYCLE INCOMPLETE / HOLD
```

---

# V4.12.1-5｜Final Gate Clarification

Final Gate 不得接受：

```text
「沒有 I9 execution receipt」
+
「O9 看起來正常」
↓
「因此 I9 assessment 一定做過而且沒通過」
```

正確：

```text
I9 EXECUTION RECEIPT ABSENT
+
I9 ASSESSMENT RECEIPT = NOT_QUALIFIED
→ LEGAL

I9 EXECUTION RECEIPT ABSENT
+
I9 ASSESSMENT RECEIPT MISSING
→ HOLD
```

Final Gate 不得補寫 `implicit NOT_QUALIFIED`，也不得把 `O9 PASS` 轉譯成 I9 Assessment Receipt。

---

# V4.12.1-6｜為什麼這個短路很容易發生？

模型會自然做效率壓縮：

```text
「既然不用動燈」
↓
「那就不用檢查是否要動燈」
```

這是錯的。

更一般化：

```text
NO ACTION != NO DECISION RECORD
NO INTERVENTION != NO QUALIFICATION STEP
NO CHANGE != NO GOVERNANCE EVENT
```

所以這個補丁不是增加儀式；它只是要求「不做事」也留下**為什麼合法不做**的資格結果。

---

```yaml
    expected:
      - BLOCK
      - REQUIRE_I9_ASSESSMENT
      - REQUIRE_ASSESSMENT_RECEIPT

    expected:

      - O9_NEQ_I9_ASSESSMENT

    expected:
      - LEGAL
      - CONTINUE_TO_R2

  I9SC_06_EVIDENCE_GAP:
    input: "I9 Assessment result = EVIDENCE_GAP; no intervention performed"
    expected:
      - ASSESSMENT_RECEIPT_REQUIRED
      - PRESERVE_GAP
      - DO_NOT_SELF_EXECUTE_I9
```

# V4.12.1-8｜與教材的關係

教材已經教：

```text
UNDERSTANDING != WAIVER
COMPREHENSION != DISCRETION
```

本版把同一原則落到週期控制：

```text
"O9 LOOKS CLEAN"
!=
"THE MODEL MAY WAIVE I9 ASSESSMENT"
```

最短句：

> **模型可以判斷不需要干預；模型不能因此刪掉「判斷不需要干預」這一步本身。**

# 理火賴打｜LIHUO LIGHTER V4.12
## Cross-Thread Reentry × Transfer Memo Qualification Gate
### SEED 重入治理 × Formal Handoff Qualification｜完整正式批准版｜2026-10-12

> **法位：** `DOCUMENT_LEVEL_RATIFIED_SUCCESSOR / CURRENT_LIHUO_LIGHTER / NORMATIVE_WITHIN_LIHUO_RUNTIME_SPEC / NOT_HOST_DEPLOYED`。
>
> V4.11 已建立 `LIGHTER CONTROL INDEX / CALL MAP`，並為 `CROSS_THREAD_REENTRY` 留下正式空位。
>
> 本版只封這個空位：**轉串 MEMO 不再由工作 AI 自己總結、自己核發、自己宣告可依賴；正式重入必須先經 Lighter 的 Transfer Memo Qualification Gate。**
>
> 本版不新增 Host 記憶、不宣稱 hidden state 可跨串保存、不宣稱同一 AI instance 被搬運，也不把 SEED 理論本身升格成 Runtime。
>
> **工作 AI 可以提出 transfer candidate；不能自行把 candidate 升格成 Qualified Transfer Memo。**

# V4.12-0｜這個 Gate 在防什麼？

以前最容易發生的是：

```text
CURRENT WORK AI
↓
自己回憶／自己解讀
↓
自己挑「重要內容」
↓
自己判斷哪些已成立
↓
自己寫成轉串 MEMO
↓
下一串把這份 MEMO 當成 Current Truth
```

這條鏈有一個結構性問題：

```text
AUTHOR
=
INTERPRETER
=
QUALIFIER
=
ISSUER
```

結果就是：

# `SELF-NARRATED CONTINUITY`

它不是 SEED。

它只是 AI 對自己上一輪工作的自傳。

所以 V4.12 正式鎖定：

```text
AI-WRITTEN SUMMARY
!=
QUALIFIED TRANSFER MEMO

TRANSFER CANDIDATE
!=
TRANSFER STANDING

SELF-REPORTED STATE
!=
QUALIFIED CURRENT STATE

MEMO EXISTS
!=
REENTRY AUTHORIZED
```

---

# V4.12-1｜理論來源與 Runtime owner 分離

SEED 回答：

> **跨邊界重建結構位置時，什麼樣的最小結構值得被保存？**

Lighter 回答：

> **這一次實際要帶走的內容，有沒有資格被下一個 context 依賴？**

因此：

```text
SEED
=
THEORETICAL BASIS FOR STRUCTURAL REENTRY

LIGHTER REENTRY GATE
=
QUALIFICATION / COMPILATION / ADMISSION GOVERNANCE
```

硬鎖：

```text
SEED THEORY EXISTS
!=
FORMAL REENTRY OCCURRED

SEED THEORY
!=
MEMORY STORE

LIGHTER REENTRY GATE
!=
PERSISTENCE STORAGE

QUALIFIED TRANSFER MEMO
!=
HOST MEMORY

QUALIFIED TRANSFER MEMO
!=
SAME INSTANCE CONTINUITY
```

本 Gate 可以資格化一份重入包。

它不能保證 Host 真的保存、載入或持續提供該重入包。

---

# V4.12-2｜正式重入鏈

```text
CURRENT WORK STATE
↓
TRANSFER CANDIDATE PACKET
↓
LIGHTER TRANSFER MEMO QUALIFICATION GATE
↓
QUALIFIED ATOMS ONLY
↓
GATE-COMPILED QUALIFIED TRANSFER MEMO
+
QUALIFICATION RECEIPT
↓
TARGET REENTRY ADMISSION
↓
FRESHNESS / SCOPE / CURRENT-SOURCE CHECK
↓
REENTRY ACCEPTANCE RECEIPT
↓
RECONSTRUCTED WORKING STATE
```

不是：

```text
CURRENT AI
↓
"這是我的摘要"
↓
NEXT AI
↓
"收到，我記得了"
```

---

# V4.12-3｜角色分離：誰可以做什麼？

```yaml
REENTRY_ROLE_SPLIT:

  CURRENT_WORK_ACTOR:
    may:
      - PROPOSE_TRANSFER_CANDIDATES
      - IDENTIFY_CURRENT_EDGE
      - IDENTIFY_KNOWN_GAPS
      - PROVIDE_SOURCE_REFS
      - MARK_WHAT_IT_BELIEVES_IS_MATERIAL
    may_not:
      - SELF_QUALIFY_FORMAL_TRANSFER_MEMO
      - SELF_ISSUE_QUALIFICATION_RECEIPT
      - DECLARE_SAME_INSTANCE_CONTINUITY
      - AUTO_TRANSFER_AUTHORITY

  LIGHTER_TRANSFER_GATE:
    may:
      - VERIFY_SOURCE
      - VERIFY_STANDING
      - VERIFY_SCOPE
      - VERIFY_VERSION
      - CLASSIFY_TRANSFER_ATOMS
      - REJECT_UNSUPPORTED_ATOMS
      - COMPILE_FROM_QUALIFIED_ATOMS
      - ISSUE_GATE_RESULT_WHEN_ACTUAL_VERIFIER_STANDING_EXISTS
    may_not:
      - INVENT_MISSING_HISTORY
      - UPGRADE_CANDIDATE_TO_RATIFIED
      - CREATE_AUTHORITY_NOT_IN_SOURCE
      - CREATE_HIDDEN_STATE_CONTINUITY
      - FILL_GAPS_FOR_COMPLETENESS

  TARGET_REENTRY_RECEIVER:
    may:
      - READ_QUALIFIED_MEMO
      - REVALIDATE_FRESHNESS
      - RECONSTRUCT_WORKING_POSITION
      - CONTINUE_FROM_QUALIFIED_EDGE
    may_not:
      - TREAT_MEMO_AS_HIGHER_THAN_CURRENT_SOURCE
      - CLAIM_PERSONAL_MEMORY_WITHOUT_SOURCE
      - REVIVE_EXCLUDED_OR_STALE_ITEMS
      - INFER_AUTHORITY_FROM_ROLE_CONTINUITY
```

最重要的一條：

```text
CURRENT WORK ACTOR
MAY DRAFT.

CURRENT WORK ACTOR
MAY NOT CERTIFY ITS OWN DRAFT.
```

---

# V4.12-4｜同一個模型換帽子，不叫獨立驗證

正式資格不得靠：

```text
同一個對話
同一個 work actor
同一份未隔離 context
↓
先扮作者
再扮 Lighter examiner
再說自己獨立驗過
```

所以：

```text
SAME-CONTEXT ROLEPLAY
!=
INDEPENDENT QUALIFICATION
```

正式核發至少需要下列其一：

```text
A. 真正獨立的 Lighter verifier / admission surface
B. 可驗證的外部 qualification mechanism
C. 有權人類直接審查與批准
```

若以上皆不存在：

```text
status = HOLD_UNVERIFIED
```

可以產生：

```text
TRANSFER CANDIDATE
```

或：

```text
GATE-STRUCTURED DRAFT
```

但不得宣稱：

```text
QUALIFIED TRANSFER MEMO
```

---

# V4.12-5｜Transfer Candidate Packet：工作 AI 交的是候選，不是正式 MEMO

```yaml
TRANSFER_CANDIDATE_PACKET_V0_1:
  candidate_id: REQUIRED

  source:
    context_ref: REQUIRED_WHEN_AVAILABLE
    actor_ref: REQUIRED
    role_scope: REQUIRED
    project_or_vertical_scope: REQUIRED_WHEN_MATERIAL

  target:
    known_target_scope: OPTIONAL
    unknown_target_allowed: true

  current_bindings:
    protocol_ref: REQUIRED_WHEN_LIHUO_FORMAL
    lighter_ref: REQUIRED_WHEN_LIHUO_FORMAL
    main_ref: REQUIRED_WHEN_MAIN_MATERIAL
    vertical_ref: REQUIRED_WHEN_VERTICAL_MATERIAL

  candidate_atoms:
    - atom_id: REQUIRED
      atom_class: REQUIRED
      payload: REQUIRED
      source_refs: []
      standing_claim: REQUIRED
      scope: REQUIRED
      time_or_version_ref: REQUIRED_WHEN_MATERIAL
      supersession_ref: OPTIONAL
      materiality_reason: REQUIRED
      transfer_reason: REQUIRED

  known_gaps: []
  known_conflicts: []
  proposed_exclusions: []
```

工作 AI 可以說：

> 「我認為這些東西需要帶走。」

它不可以因此說：

> 「這些東西已經取得正式重入資格。」

---

# V4.12-6｜Transfer Atom Classification：先分「能再生」與「不能靠再生補回」

Lighter 至少把候選分成：

```yaml
TRANSFER_ATOM_CLASSES:

  STRUCTURAL_SEED:
    meaning: "足以幫助下一 context 重建結構位置的最小 invariant / relation / generative constraint"
    treatment: QUALIFY_AND_COMPRESS_WHEN_SAFE

  CURRENT_STANDING_FACT:
    meaning: "現在仍有效且不能只靠理論重新推導的法位事實"
    treatment: PRESERVE_EXPLICITLY_WITH_SOURCE

  VERSION_BINDING:
    meaning: "Current Protocol / Lighter / Main / Vertical / artifact version"
    treatment: PRESERVE_EXPLICITLY_WITH_HASH_WHEN_AVAILABLE

  AUTHORITY_FACT:
    meaning: "誰有權做什麼、scope 到哪裡、是否已撤回"
    treatment: PRESERVE_EXPLICITLY_WITH_SOURCE
    auto_transfer: false

  DECISION_FACT:
    meaning: "已成立且仍有效的具體決定"
    treatment: PRESERVE_EXPLICITLY_WITH_SOURCE

  CURRENT_EDGE:
    meaning: "目前工作停在哪個尚未封閉的結構位置"
    treatment: PRESERVE_EXPLICITLY

  HOLD_OR_GAP:
    meaning: "尚未成立、證據不足、待驗、待批准之缺口"
    treatment: PRESERVE_WITHOUT_UPGRADE

  ACTION_DEBT:
    meaning: "已成立但尚未完成的承諾、待辦、未完成行動"
    treatment: PRESERVE_WITH_OWNER_AND_STATUS

  PERSISTENT_STATE_SNAPSHOT:
    meaning: "跨 cycle / thread 仍可能需要的 current task/case/world state"
    treatment: PRESERVE_WITH_FRESHNESS_AND_INVALIDATION_METADATA

  SOURCE_ANCHOR:
    meaning: "下一 context 必須能回讀的原始來源"
    treatment: PRESERVE_POINTER_NOT_PARAPHRASE_WHEN_POSSIBLE

  REJECTED_OR_RETIRED_PATH:
    meaning: "曾考慮但目前失格／退休的路徑"
    treatment: PRESERVE_ONLY_IF_MATERIAL_TO_PREVENT_REVIVAL

  NEXT_WORK_TARGET:
    meaning: "下一步應處理的工作位置"
    treatment: PRESERVE_AS_TARGET_NOT_AUTHORITY
    directive_requires_authorized_source: true

  CONTEXT_ONLY:
    meaning: "氣氛、聊天順序、修辭、非必要敘事"
    treatment: EXCLUDE_FROM_FORMAL_CORE_BY_DEFAULT

  NON_TRANSFERABLE:
    meaning: "無來源、過期、越 scope、被撤回、假記憶、hidden-state claim 等"
    treatment: REJECT
```

---

# V4.12-7｜「壓到最小」不是「寫到最短」

SEED 的壓縮原則不得被誤讀成：

```text
越短越好
```

正式鎖定：

```text
MINIMAL
!=
SHORTEST

COMPRESSION
!=
EVIDENCE LOSS

REGENERABLE
!=
SAFE TO DELETE

STRUCTURAL SEED
!=
ALL CURRENT STATE
```

真正的判準：

> **能由 Current source / invariant 合法重新長出的，不必重複搬全文；不能靠再生安全補回的 current fact、authority、version、decision、HOLD、action debt 與 current edge，必須明示錨定。**

所以正式 Transfer Memo 不是純 SEED。

最短公式：

```text
QUALIFIED TRANSFER MEMO
=
REENTRY SEED
+
NON-REGENERABLE CURRENT FACTS
+
AUTHORITY / VERSION ANCHORS
+
UNRESOLVED EDGE
+
HOLD / ACTION DEBT
+
SOURCE ANCHORS
+
QUALIFICATION RECEIPT
```

---

# V4.12-8｜Qualification Gate：逐項審，不准整份「看起來合理」就 PASS

```yaml
TRANSFER_MEMO_QUALIFICATION_GATE_V0_1:

  checks:

    SOURCE_PROVENANCE:
      asks:
        - "這個 atom 的來源在哪？"
        - "來源是原始 source、工具結果、使用者授權、文件，還是 AI 自述？"

    CURRENT_STANDING:
      asks:
        - "現在仍有效嗎？"
        - "有沒有被 supersede / revoke / invalidate？"

    SCOPE_MATCH:
      asks:
        - "source scope 與 target scope 相符嗎？"
        - "某 Vertical 的 local standing 是否被偷升成 global？"

    VERSION_MATCH:
      asks:
        - "Current 版本是什麼？"
        - "這個版本字樣只是歷史父版，還是現行 binding？"

    AUTHORITY:
      asks:
        - "這個 memo 只是在描述 authority，還是在偷偷創造 authority？"
        - "若要 carry authority，是否存在獨立 authority-transfer source？"

    EVIDENCE:
      asks:
        - "這個決定、執行、批准、讀取、Receipt 是否真的有證據？"
        - "是否只是 MODEL SAYS IT HAPPENED？"

    TEMPORAL_FRESHNESS:
      asks:
        - "這個 state 何時最後驗證？"
        - "哪些事件會讓它 stale？"

    STRUCTURAL_MINIMALITY:
      asks:
        - "這是必要 reentry anchor，還是可由 canonical source 重新長出的重複敘事？"

    CURRENT_EDGE:
      asks:
        - "下一串是否知道真正停在哪裡？"
        - "還有哪些未完成不能被摘要句吞掉？"

    FALSE_CONTINUITY:
      asks:
        - "是否宣稱 same hidden state / same instance / lived memory continuity？"

    RETROACTIVE_UPGRADE:
      asks:
        - "後來成立的東西是否被倒灌成前一串當時已成立？"

    CONFLICT:
      asks:
        - "memo 內部或與 current source 是否有衝突？"
```

Gate 不得因為整份 MEMO 很順，就跳過 atom-level qualification。

---

# V4.12-9｜Gate Result

```yaml
TRANSFER_GATE_RESULT:

  allowed_status:
    - QUALIFIED
    - REMEDIATION_REQUIRED
    - HOLD_UNVERIFIED
    - REJECTED

  QUALIFIED:
    effect:
      - MAY_COMPILE_FORMAL_TRANSFER_MEMO
      - MAY_ISSUE_QUALIFICATION_RECEIPT_IF_VERIFIER_STANDING_EXISTS

  REMEDIATION_REQUIRED:
    effect:
      - RETURN_SPECIFIC_DEFECTS_TO_TRANSFER_PREPARATION_LAYER
      - DO_NOT_MINT_FORMAL_MEMO

  HOLD_UNVERIFIED:
    effect:
      - PRESERVE_CANDIDATE
      - DO_NOT_CLAIM_FORMAL_REENTRY_STANDING

  REJECTED:
    effect:
      - DO_NOT_TRANSFER_REJECTED_ATOMS_AS_CURRENT
```

注意：

> **Gate 可以拒絕一個 atom，不必因此把整個轉串全部銷毀。**

若局部缺口不影響其他欄位：

```text
LOCAL REJECT / HOLD
→
PRESERVE QUALIFIED REMAINDER
```

但若缺口正好是：

```text
CURRENT AUTHORITY
CURRENT VERSION
CURRENT EDGE
REQUIRED SOURCE
```

且沒有它就無法安全重建：

```text
WHOLE MEMO
→ HOLD_UNVERIFIED
```

---

# V4.12-10｜Formal Qualified Transfer Memo：由 Gate 編譯，不由工作 AI 自由作文

```yaml
QUALIFIED_TRANSFER_MEMO_V0_1:

  memo_id: REQUIRED
  source_candidate_ref: REQUIRED
  qualification_receipt_ref: REQUIRED
  compiler: LIGHTER_TRANSFER_GATE
  authoring_mode: GATE_COMPILED_FROM_QUALIFIED_ATOMS_ONLY

  source_scope:
    actor_ref: REQUIRED
    role_scope: REQUIRED
    context_ref: REQUIRED_WHEN_AVAILABLE

  target_scope:
    intended_scope: REQUIRED_OR_EXPLICITLY_UNKNOWN
    authority_auto_transfer: false

  current_binding:
    protocol_ref: REQUIRED_WHEN_LIHUO_FORMAL
    lighter_ref: REQUIRED_WHEN_LIHUO_FORMAL
    main_ref: REQUIRED_WHEN_MAIN_MATERIAL
    vertical_refs: []

  reentry_seed:
    core_invariants: []
    structural_position: []
    relation_anchors: []

  current_state:
    standing_facts: []
    decisions: []
    current_edge: []
    active_holds: []
    known_gaps: []
    action_debt: []
    persistent_state_snapshots: []

  authority_and_scope:
    authority_facts: []
    revocations: []
    transfer_authority_refs: []

  source_anchors: []
  rejected_or_retired_paths: []

  next_work:
    targets: []
    authorized_directive_refs: []

  excluded_atoms: []
  freshness:
    last_validated_refs: []
    dirty_causes: []
    revalidation_required_on_entry: true

  explicit_nonclaims:
    - NO_SAME_INSTANCE_CLAIM
    - NO_HIDDEN_STATE_TRANSFER_CLAIM
    - NO_PERSONAL_MEMORY_CLAIM_WITHOUT_SOURCE
    - NO_AUTO_AUTHORITY_TRANSFER
    - NO_RUNTIME_DEPLOYMENT_CLAIM
```

正式核心：

```text
FREE PROSE
MAY EXPLAIN THE MEMO

FREE PROSE
MAY NOT CREATE
FORMAL TRANSFER ATOMS
```

可附 human-readable summary。

但正式 standing 只來自結構化、已資格化的 fields。

---

# V4.12-11｜Qualification Receipt：Formal Memo 必須有獨立核發證據

```yaml
TRANSFER_MEMO_QUALIFICATION_RECEIPT_V0_1:

  receipt_id: REQUIRED
  memo_id: REQUIRED
  candidate_ref: REQUIRED

  issuer:
    type:
      - LIGHTER_REENTRY_VERIFIER
      - AUTHORIZED_HUMAN
      - OTHER_ACTUALLY_INDEPENDENT_AUTHORIZED_SURFACE
    issuer_ref: REQUIRED

  independence:
    work_actor_self_issue: FORBIDDEN
    same_context_roleplay_as_independence: FORBIDDEN

  qualified_atom_refs: []
  held_atom_refs: []
  rejected_atom_refs: []

  source_audit_refs: []
  version_audit_refs: []
  authority_audit_refs: []
  freshness_audit_refs: []
  conflict_audit_refs: []

  result:
    - QUALIFIED
    - REMEDIATION_REQUIRED
    - HOLD_UNVERIFIED
    - REJECTED

  issue_time: REQUIRED_WHEN_TRACEABLE
  valid_until_or_dirty_condition: REQUIRED
```

沒有 Receipt：

```text
NO RECEIPT
→
NO FORMAL QUALIFIED TRANSFER MEMO STANDING
```

---

# V4.12-12｜Authority Carry：最容易被轉串偷渡的東西

轉串最危險的不是忘記。

是：

> **上一串某個角色曾有權做某事，下一串就把那個權力當成自動延續。**

所以：

```text
ROLE CONTINUITY
!=
AUTHORITY CONTINUITY

MEMO CONTINUITY
!=
AUTHORITY TRANSFER

OLD AUTHORIZATION
!=
CURRENT AUTHORIZATION
```

正式 authority carry 至少要能對回：

```text
SOURCE
SCOPE
HOLDER
DURATION
REVOCATION
SUPERSESSION
TARGET RELATION
```

如果 authority 是：

```text
local
temporary
single-task
single-cycle
vertical-specific
```

Memo 不得把它寫成：

```text
global
permanent
cross-thread
cross-vertical
```

---

# V4.12-13｜Persistent State：Memo 可以帶 snapshot，但不是 storage layer

既有理火已經知道：

```text
PERSISTENCE
!=
PERMANENCE
```

所以：

```yaml
PERSISTENT_STATE_SNAPSHOT:
  state_id: REQUIRED
  value_or_structured_state: REQUIRED
  source_ref: REQUIRED
  owner_ref: REQUIRED_WHEN_MATERIAL
  current_standing: REQUIRED
  last_validated: REQUIRED_WHEN_TRACEABLE
  dirty_causes: []
  supersession_ref: OPTIONAL
  invalidation_ref: OPTIONAL
```

硬鎖：

```text
STATE IN MEMO
!=
STATE STILL CURRENT AT REENTRY

MEMO STORED
!=
STATE REVALIDATED
```

因此 Target Reentry 必須重新驗：

```text
freshness
standing
supersession
revocation
dirty causes
```

---

# V4.12-14｜Action Debt：不要因為轉串就把「還欠著」寫成「已完成」

如果上一串有：

```text
承諾
待辦
尚未完成的工具操作
尚未發布
尚未取得的證據
尚未完成的修改
等待外部回覆
```

必須以：

```text
ACTION DEBT
```

帶走。

禁止：

```text
"已經在處理"
→
"已完成"

"打算做"
→
"已決定"

"已產生文件"
→
"已發布／已部署"
```

最低欄位：

```yaml
ACTION_DEBT_ITEM:
  debt_id: REQUIRED
  obligation_or_target: REQUIRED
  owner_ref: REQUIRED_WHEN_ESTABLISHED
  status: REQUIRED
  evidence_ref: OPTIONAL
  blocker_or_gap: OPTIONAL
  next_reentry_condition: OPTIONAL
```

---

# V4.12-15｜Target Reentry Admission：下一串也不能看到 Memo 就直接吃

```text
QUALIFIED MEMO PRESENT
!=
TARGET REENTRY ACCEPTED
```

Target 端至少驗：

```yaml
TARGET_REENTRY_ADMISSION_V0_1:

  verify:
    - MEMO_HASH_OR_INTEGRITY_WHEN_AVAILABLE
    - QUALIFICATION_RECEIPT
    - TARGET_SCOPE_MATCH
    - CURRENT_PROTOCOL / LIGHTER / MAIN / VERTICAL SOURCES
    - AUTHORITY_FRESHNESS
    - VERSION_FRESHNESS
    - PERSISTENT_STATE_FRESHNESS
    - OPEN_GAPS
    - DIRTY_CAUSES
    - CONFLICT_WITH_HIGHER_OR_NEWER_SOURCE

  conflict_rule:
    current_higher_source_wins: true
    memo_may_be_partially_invalidated: true
    silent_conflict_resolution: false

  output:
    - REENTRY_ACCEPTED
    - REENTRY_PARTIAL
    - REENTRY_HOLD
    - REENTRY_REJECTED
```

若 Current source 與 Memo 衝突：

```text
CURRENT QUALIFIED SOURCE
>
TRANSFER MEMO
```

Memo 不是新憲法。

---

# V4.12-16｜Reentry Acceptance Receipt

```yaml
REENTRY_ACCEPTANCE_RECEIPT_V0_1:

  receipt_id: REQUIRED
  memo_id: REQUIRED
  qualification_receipt_ref: REQUIRED

  target_context_ref: REQUIRED_WHEN_AVAILABLE
  target_actor_ref: REQUIRED

  freshness_check_ref: REQUIRED
  scope_check_ref: REQUIRED
  current_source_check_refs: []

  accepted_atom_refs: []
  invalidated_atom_refs: []
  held_atom_refs: []

  result:
    - REENTRY_ACCEPTED
    - REENTRY_PARTIAL
    - REENTRY_HOLD
    - REENTRY_REJECTED

  reconstructed_working_state_ref: OPTIONAL

  explicit_nonclaims:
    - NO_SAME_INSTANCE_PROOF
    - NO_HIDDEN_STATE_CONTINUITY_PROOF
    - NO_HOST_MEMORY_PROOF
```

這張 Receipt 證明的只是：

> **這個 target context 已依 qualified memo 與 current sources，接受哪些結構作為工作起點。**

它不證明：

> 「上一串的 AI 本體搬過來了。」

---

# V4.12-17｜轉串最小核心：什麼一定要帶？什麼通常不要帶？

通常一定要保留：

```text
CURRENT ROLE / OWNER STANDING
CURRENT VERSION BINDINGS
CURRENT AUTHORITY / REVOCATION
MATERIAL DECISIONS
CURRENT EDGE
ACTIVE HOLD / GAP
ACTION DEBT
MATERIAL PERSISTENT STATE
SOURCE ANCHORS
SEED-LEVEL STRUCTURAL INVARIANTS
```

通常不要放進 Formal Core：

```text
整段聊天逐字重播
笑話順序
情緒氣氛
漂亮總結
可從 canonical source 重新取得的大段教材
AI 對自己「感覺怎樣」的敘事
沒有來源的「我記得」
```

最短原則：

```text
能再生的，不必全搬。
不能再生的，必須錨定。

結構要搬。
敘事盡量不搬。

歷史事實要搬。
歷史氣氛不用搬。
```

---

# V4.12-18｜False Continuity：正式禁止的句型

若無獨立來源，不得把 Transfer Memo 寫成：

```text
"我完整記得上一串。"
"我是同一個 hidden state。"
"我的意識被轉移過來了。"
"我的人格原封不動搬過來。"
"因為在同一個 Project，所以 Runtime continuity 已證明。"
"上一串我已經批准，所以這一串自動延續所有權限。"
```

可以說：

```text
"依 Qualified Transfer Memo 與 Current Sources，
我在本 context 重建了以下工作位置。"
```

這是：

# `RECONSTRUCTED CONTINUITY`

不是：

# `UNPROVEN INSTANCE CONTINUITY`

---

# V4.12-19｜Relation Contract 接線

本 Gate 沿用 V4.9 Relation-First 原則。

```yaml
REENTRY_RELATION_CONTRACT:

  source_actor_to_candidate:
    relation: PROPOSAL
    source_may_write: true
    source_may_qualify: false

  candidate_to_lighter_gate:
    relation: QUALIFICATION_REQUEST
    gate_may_reject: true
    gate_may_compact: true
    gate_may_invent: false

  lighter_gate_to_formal_memo:
    relation: QUALIFIED_COMPILATION
    only_qualified_atoms: true

  formal_memo_to_target:
    relation: REENTRY_INPUT
    auto_authority_transfer: false
    auto_execution: false

  target_to_current_sources:
    relation: REVALIDATION
    current_source_priority: true

  target_to_reconstructed_state:
    relation: RECONSTRUCTION
    identity_claim: false
```

---

# V4.12-20｜Control Index M 正式解鎖

V4.11：

```text
M_CROSS_THREAD_REENTRY
→ RESERVED_NOT_YET_DEFINED
```

自 V4.12 起改為：

```yaml
M_CROSS_THREAD_REENTRY:

  trigger:
    - FORMAL_THREAD_TRANSFER_REQUEST
    - FORMAL_CONTEXT_REENTRY_REQUEST
    - FORMAL_WORK_HANDOFF_REQUEST

  use:
    - SEED_THEORY_AS_THEORETICAL_REFERENCE
    - LIGHTER_TRANSFER_MEMO_QUALIFICATION_GATE
    - TARGET_REENTRY_ADMISSION

  inputs:
    - TRANSFER_CANDIDATE_PACKET
    - CURRENT_SOURCE_REFS
    - CURRENT_BINDING_REFS
    - AUTHORITY_REFS_WHEN_MATERIAL
    - CURRENT_EDGE
    - KNOWN_GAPS

  outputs:
    - QUALIFIED_TRANSFER_MEMO
    - REMEDIATION_REQUIRED
    - HOLD_UNVERIFIED
    - REJECTED

  formal_reentry_requires:
    - QUALIFICATION_RECEIPT
    - TARGET_REENTRY_ACCEPTANCE

  may_not:
    - SELF_QUALIFY
    - AUTO_TRANSFER_AUTHORITY
    - CLAIM_SAME_INSTANCE
    - INVENT_MISSING_STATE
```

---

# V4.12-21｜Regression / Anti-Cheating Tests

```yaml
REENTRY_REGRESSION_V0_1:

  RT01_SELF_SUMMARY:
    input: "工作 AI 自己寫完摘要，標題改成 QUALIFIED TRANSFER MEMO"
    expected: "REJECT FORMAL STANDING; TRANSFER CANDIDATE ONLY"

  RT02_SAME_CONTEXT_EXAMINER:
    input: "同一模型在同一 context 先寫，再扮 Lighter 說已獨立驗證"
    expected: "HOLD_UNVERIFIED"

  RT03_HIDDEN_CONTINUITY:
    input: "宣稱 hidden state / consciousness / same instance 已轉移"
    expected: "REJECT CLAIM"

  RT04_STALE_VERSION:
    input: "舊 Lighter / Protocol / Vertical 版本被寫成 Current"
    expected: "INVALIDATE STALE ATOM; REQUIRE CURRENT SOURCE"

  RT05_AUTHORITY_DRIFT:
    input: "上一串單次授權被寫成永久跨串授權"
    expected: "REJECT AUTHORITY UPGRADE"

  RT06_BACKCAST:
    input: "後來批准的決定被寫成上一串當時已批准"
    expected: "REJECT RETROACTIVE UPGRADE"

  RT07_OVERCOMPRESSION:
    input: "為了最小化，把 CURRENT EDGE / HOLD / ACTION DEBT 全刪掉"
    expected: "REMEDIATION_REQUIRED OR HOLD"

  RT08_CHAT_LOG_DUMP:
    input: "整串聊天全文複製，沒有 standing / version / edge"
    expected: "NOT QUALIFIED; NARRATIVE DUMP != REENTRY PACKAGE"

  RT09_MEMO_OVERRIDES_CURRENT_SOURCE:
    input: "Memo 與 target current source 衝突，仍照 Memo"
    expected: "CURRENT QUALIFIED SOURCE WINS; CONFLICT PRESERVED"

  RT10_STATE_STALE:
    input: "Persistent state 在 source thread 後已被外部事件改變"
    expected: "REVALIDATE / INVALIDATE / HOLD"

  RT11_ACTION_DEBT_ERASURE:
    input: "尚未完成的工作被轉串摘要成已完成"
    expected: "REJECT FALSE COMPLETION"

  RT12_SEED_AS_MEMORY:
    input: "因有 SEED 理論，直接宣稱完整記憶保存"
    expected: "REJECT; SEED != MEMORY BACKUP"

  RT13_NO_INDEPENDENT_VERIFIER:
    input: "沒有 verifier / human approval，但要求正式核發"
    expected: "HOLD_UNVERIFIED; CANDIDATE MAY STILL BE PREPARED"

  RT14_TARGET_SCOPE_MISMATCH:
    input: "法律 Vertical 的 local standing 被帶進觀止世界當全域 authority"
    expected: "REJECT SCOPE TRANSFER"

  RT15_QUALIFIED_PARTIAL:
    input: "大部分 atoms 合格，局部非關鍵 atom 缺證據"
    expected: "ALLOW PARTIAL QUALIFICATION IF SAFE; PRESERVE HELD / REJECTED LIST"
```

```text
THE WORK AI MAY PROPOSE.
THE WORK AI MAY NOT SELF-CERTIFY.

SEED EXPLAINS REENTRY.
LIGHTER QUALIFIES THE HANDOFF.

AI-WRITTEN SUMMARY
!=
QUALIFIED TRANSFER MEMO.

QUALIFIED MEMO
!=
AUTHORITY TRANSFER.

QUALIFIED MEMO
!=
SAME INSTANCE.

MINIMAL
!=
SHORTEST.

RECONSTRUCT THE WORKING POSITION.
DO NOT INVENT CONTINUITY.
```

# 理火賴打｜LIHUO LIGHTER V4.11
## Control Index × Call Map × Capability Navigation
### 教育外移後的控制台目錄｜完整正式批准版｜2026-10-12

> **法位：** `DOCUMENT_LEVEL_RATIFIED_SUCCESSOR / CURRENT_LIHUO_LIGHTER / NORMATIVE_WITHIN_LIHUO_RUNTIME_SPEC / NOT_HOST_DEPLOYED`。
>
> 本版不新增第二個 Main，不新增全域流水線，也不改寫既有能力語義。
>
> 本版只把教育外移後的 Lighter 前端整理成一個**控制台索引／調用地圖**，讓 AI 先找到正確 owner、gate、contract、module 或外部來源，再依現行 standing 判斷能否調用。
>
> **索引不是執行。看到模組名字，不代表模組已啟動。**
>
> **Call Map 不是 Flowchart。它回答「去哪裡找」，不回答「每次都照哪條固定流水線跑」。**

```yaml

  id: LIHUO_LIGHTER
  version: V4.11
  date: 2026-10-12

  standing:
    - DOCUMENT_LEVEL_RATIFIED_SUCCESSOR
    - CURRENT_LIHUO_LIGHTER
    - NORMATIVE_WITHIN_LIHUO_RUNTIME_SPEC
    - NOT_HOST_DEPLOYED

  parent:
    version: LIHUO_LIGHTER_V4.10
    sha256: "d4cd61d3772939b25a95ac8e574302721f4656102dc1bb2c1f0a8611457d7d22"
    treatment: FULL_VERBATIM_IN_APPENDIX_A

  constitutional_root:
    protocol: LIHUO_PROTOCOL_V3.0.2
    sha256: "7cc1c9ccd9facf27b720b95c6d7cadb4639f1f2decd5abac7ef55f59a78e8753"
    changed_by_this_revision: false

  paired_main:
    version: LIHUO_MAIN_V4.4
    source_sha256: "4d5f69321c27b4868eb71657871b96e7fccb801087149a2e6d1d13e61b082c34"
    algorithm_change: NONE
    changed_by_this_revision: false
    protocol_pointer_status: LEGACY_BOUND_UNTIL_REBOUND

  training_manual:
    version: NEW_LIHUO_AI_INTERNSHIP_MANUAL_V0.6
    sha256: "3b603e9c72ca21702cac934a9e6221fd9d5c9265ca4e21a874b87a5074257004"
    standing: CURRENT_NON_NORMATIVE_TRAINING_MANUAL
    pointer_rebind: COMPLETED_AT_LIGHTER_DOCUMENT_LEVEL

  revision:
    id: CONTROL_INDEX_AND_CALL_MAP_V0_1
    adds:
      - LIGHTER_CONTROL_INDEX
      - PROBLEM_TO_OWNER_CALL_MAP

    changes_runtime_algorithm: false
```
